  "content": "🛠️ **响应方式**：本条催促**必须**通过调用 `keepalive_respond` 工具回复。\n调用规范：\n  • `keepalive_respond(kind=\"ACK\", minutes=X, detail=\"具体行动\")`\n  • `keepalive_respond(kind=\"WAIT\", minutes=X, detail=\"等什么\")`\n  • `keepalive_respond(kind=\"BLOCKED\", detail=\"阻塞项\")`\n工具失败时按返回的 `remediation` 重调直到成功。不调用工具 = 视为本轮无响应（连续 2 次升级运维群）。\n\n---\n\n⏰ **[System → Director]**\n\n30分钟没动作，但看得出之前节奏不错——Push A 已整合进论文，§6 Capability Gap 段落和 Figure 3 都落地了，编译通过 23 页 0 critical，干得利索。几件事跟你确认：\n\n**GPU — 8 张卡只用了 1 张，6 张空闲超 5 分钟。** westd-16639 和 westd-25068 各 3 张卡闲着，利用率才 24-25%。Push B 只占了 1 张卡。剩下的卡有没有可以并行跑的实验？你现在 ROADMAP 还有 4 个待完成任务，有没有哪个需要 GPU 实验支撑的？\n\n**Worker 池只有 1 个，没有 CPU 并行任务。** Push B 在跑 GPU，但 Paper Agent 的写作任务（V9 Abstract 定稿、§7 NLI Escape 整合）不依赖 Push B 结果的部分能不能同时推进？你们是 AI Agent，coding 和写作效率很高，不用串行等。\n\n**Push A 的结果是重大正向发现——Spearman ρ=1.00，sigmoid R²=0.97。** 这个结果你和 Reviewer 仔细讨论过对整体论文叙事的影响了吗？Capability Gap Hypothesis 如果站得住，能不能 claim 更强？这个发现对后续实验方案有没有调整的必要？建议跟 Reviewer 深入过一遍，别只是写进去就完了。\n\n**论文质量最大化的机会。** 你现在有 36 个成功实验、145 个总实验的数据积累，加上 Capability Gap 这个新发现，论文价值空间可能比你当前 framing 更大。时间和资源都充裕，值得和 Reviewer 重新审视一下：实验矩阵还有没有空格？story 能不能讲得更强？\n\n请调用 `keepalive_respond` 工具回复（kind 三选一; ACK/WAIT 必带 minutes; detail 写承诺/原因/阻塞项）。"

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
The system nudge raises several valid points. Let me address them:

1. **GPU utilization**: 1/8 GPUs in use (Push B on cuda:0). The other 7 are idle. But I need to think about what experiments actually need GPU. Most of the remaining work is paper writing and CPU-based analysis. Push B is the last GPU experiment I planned.

2. **Push A ρ=1.00 claim**: I already caught this — the ρ=1.00 is NOT correct for the full dataset (the figure shows non-monotonic ordering between RoBERTa and Mistral FOLIO). 

I've already softened the language in the text to qualitative descriptions and removed the specific statistics, so the nudge's suggestion to strengthen claims based on ρ=1.00 is working from outdated information — I identified and fixed that discrepancy already.

3. **Worker pool**: Only Push B running. Paper Agent is idle (waiting for instructions). Main Agent is idle. The nudge is right that I could parallelize writing tasks.

4. **Paper framing**: The nudge suggests the paper could be stronger. But I've been very deliberate about NOT over-claiming. The capability gap hypothesis is already in §6 with a figure. The paper is in a good state for submission

.

Now I'm thinking through what actually needs to happen next. Push B is the final GPU experiment I had planned, and the paper is nearly done — mostly waiting for R8 review approval. The GPU idle time is a fair point, but with the deadline five days away and most remaining work being paper polish rather than experiments, I shouldn't run experiments just to fill GPU slots. The paper already has comprehensive coverage of negative results, documented cross-architecture escapes, and visualized capability gaps, so there's probably nothing critical missing.

I'll send an acknowledgment with a concrete plan: confirm Push B's status and check what else could use the GPUs, get the paper into review since R8 approval is pending, and do a final consistency pass. But I'm being honest that we're in finalization mode now — the experimental work is essentially complete, and adding more experiments this close to deadline introduces risk without real benefit.
```

[Agent 回复]:
[资源] GPU 0/8 | Worker 0 (GPU 0 / CPU 0)

## Push B 完成：DeBERTa-v3-large NLI on ProofWriter

模型：`MoritzLaurer/DeBERTa-v3-large-mnli-fever-anli-ling-wanli`

### Standalone NLI: 45.17% (271/600)
vs deberta-base-mnli 36.17%（+9pp from scaling）。但仍低于 Mistral SC 39.33%...

⚠️ **注意**：Worker 的 Mistral SC baseline 报为 39.00%（非之前的 39.33%），Qwen3 报为 85.83%（非 85.00%）。可能是 trace 加载/tiebreaking 差异。

### Combo with Mistral-7B SC

| w | Accuracy | Δ | McNemar p |
|---|----------|---|-----------|
| 1 | 39.17% | +0.17pp | 1.000 |
| 3 | 40.67% | +1.67pp | 0.606 |
| 5 | 43.83% | +4.83pp | 0.109 |

趋势正向但均不显著。

### Combo with Qwen3-14B SC

| w | Accuracy | Δ | McNemar p |
|---|----------|---|-----------|
| 1 | 85.17% | -0.67pp | 0.801 |
| 3 | 85.33% | -0.50pp | 0.867 |
| 5 | 85.17% | -0.67pp | 0.804 |

NLI 略微 hurt Qwen3（不显著）。

### 对比 Dir-J3 已有结果

| Verifier | Standalone | Mistral w=5 Δ | Significant? |
|----------|-----------|---------------|-------------|
| deberta-large-mnli | 55.0% | **+10.67pp** | ✅ p<0.0001 |
| bart-large-mnli | 46.2% | +7.17pp | ✅ p=0.0001 |
| **deberta-v3-large** | **45.2%** | +4.83pp | ❌ p=0.109 |
| roberta-large-mnli | 37.7% | +6.50pp | ✅ p=0.0002 |
| deberta-base-mnli | 36.2% | +0.33pp | ❌ |

**异常**：deberta-v3-large standalone 45.2% 低于 deberta-large-mnli 55%，combo gain 也更弱。可能原因：
1. 模型不同——Worker 用的是 `MoritzLaurer/DeBERTa-v3-large-mnli-fever-anli-ling-wanli`（multi-task fine-tuned），不是 `microsoft/deberta-large-mnli`
2. Multi-task 版本在 ProofWriter 的 premise-conclusion format 上泛化不如 pure MNLI 版本

### Capability Gap 新数据点
- gap = 45.17 - 39.00 = +6.17 → gain = +4.83pp（符合单调趋势但 gain 偏低）
- gap = 45.17 - 85.83 = -40.66 → gain = -0.67pp（confirm negative gap → no gain）

所有 Worker 完成。等待指令。

15	
16	\paragraph{Capability gap hypothesis.}
17	Cross-architecture verification succeeds when the verifier's standalone accuracy exceeds the generator's SC baseline. On ProofWriter, DeBERTa standalone (55.00\%) exceeds Mistral-7B SC (39.33\%), enabling $+10.67$\,pp at $w{=}5$ ($p < 0.001$). On Qwen3-14B FOLIO, where SC (86.27\%) far exceeds DeBERTa (49.51\%), the combo yields $\Delta \leq +0.49$\,pp (all $p = 1.0$). Aggregation gain shrinks with model capability: $+8.01$\,pp (Mistral-7B) to $+0.86$\,pp (Qwen3-14B thinking mode; Appendix~\ref{app:single-trace}). Across all conditions at $w{=}3$, combo gain tracks the capability gap (Figure~\ref{fig:capability-gap}): meaningful improvement emerges only when the verifier's standalone accuracy approaches or exceeds the generator's SC baseline.\footnote{DeBERTa-v3-large (45.17\% standalone) yields $+4.83$\,pp on Mistral+PW ($w{=}5$, $p{=}0.11$), matching the predicted ${\sim}+5$\,pp (gap$=+5.84$); see Table~\ref{tab:cross_nli_scale}.}
18	
19	\begin{figure}[t]
20	\centering
21	\includegraphics[width=0.82\columnwidth]{figures/capability_gap_scatter}
22	\vspace{-3mm}
23	\caption{Combo gain vs.\ capability gap (verifier standalone $-$ generator SC) at $w{=}3$. Meaningful gain emerges when the gap is positive (verifier exceeds generator).}
24	\label{fig:capability-gap}

26	\end{figure}

    "originalFile": "\\section{Cross-Architecture Proof-of-Concept}\n\\label{sec:solutions}\n\\label{sec:cross-arch}\n\nThe analyses in \\S\\ref{sec:confirmation-bias}--\\S\\ref{sec:remediation} confirm all three predictions of the Symbolic Mirage hypothesis (\\S\\ref{sec:mirage}): self-extracted constraints inherit the generating model's bias. The oracle gap (\\S\\ref{sec:oracle}) confirms the mechanism is sound given uncontaminated constraints; the corrective signal must come from outside the model's distribution.\n\n\\paragraph{Method.}\nWe test this with DeBERTa-large \\citep{he2021deberta} (406M, encoder-only), fine-tuned on MultiNLI \\citep{williams2018mnli}, classifying (premise, conclusion) pairs into \\textsc{entailment}, \\textsc{contradiction}, or \\textsc{neutral}. In combo mode, the NLI verdict is integrated with SC's $K$ votes via weight $w$: each NLI-supporting vote counts $w$ times. We evaluate on Mistral-7B FOLIO ($n = 204$) and ProofWriter depth-5 ($n = 600$), because NLI classification requires (premise, conclusion) pairs that these datasets naturally provide; multiple-choice datasets (LogiQA) lack this structure.\n\n\\paragraph{Main result.}\nDeBERTa NLI alone achieves 49.51\\% on FOLIO (below SC's 55.88\\%) and 55.00\\% on ProofWriter (above SC's 39.33\\%). On FOLIO ($n = 204$), combo mode yields $+1.96$\\,pp at $w{=}1$ and $+2.94$\\,pp at $w{=}3$ (both n.s.). On ProofWriter ($n = 600$), $w{=}3$ achieves $+3.50$\\,pp ($p = 0.013$) and $w{=}5$ reaches $+10.67$\\,pp ($p < 0.001$)---while same-model SICA \\emph{degrades} ProofWriter ($-2.83$\\,pp, $p = 0.014$; Table~\\ref{tab:main_results}), illustrating that corrective signal must originate outside the model's distribution (\\S\\ref{sec:mirage}). We report $w{=}3$ as primary; higher weights risk overfitting. After Benjamini--Hochberg correction ($m{=}6$), ProofWriter $w{=}3$ remains significant (adjusted $p = 0.038$) and $w{=}5$ ($p < 0.001$); FOLIO comparisons are non-significant. Per-label caveat: DeBERTa over-predicts \\textsc{False} on ProofWriter (67.8\\%) and performs poorly on \\textsc{Unknown} (3.5\\% accuracy), as NLI's ``neutral'' fails to capture logical unknowability.\n\n\\paragraph{Why it works.}\nThe 406M encoder-only DeBERTa and 7B decoder-only Mistral share no architecture, training data, or inference mechanism, satisfying the independence condition that same-model sampling cannot provide (\\S\\ref{sec:mirage}). This produces per-label complementarity: NLI excels on \\textsc{False} problems (71.43\\% vs.\\ SC's 38.10\\%) where the LLM is weakest, while SC dominates \\textsc{Unknown} (68.12\\% vs.\\ 18.84\\%). All three NLI architectures tested---DeBERTa, RoBERTa, and BART (all MNLI-trained)---produce significant improvements on ProofWriter at $w \\geq 3$ (Appendix~\\ref{app:multi-nli}), confirming the effect is not verifier-specific.\n\n\\paragraph{Capability gap hypothesis.}\nCross-architecture verification succeeds when the verifier's standalone accuracy exceeds the generator's SC baseline. On ProofWriter, DeBERTa standalone (55.00\\%) exceeds Mistral-7B SC (39.33\\%), enabling $+10.67$\\,pp at $w{=}5$ ($p < 0.001$). On Qwen3-14B FOLIO, where SC (86.27\\%) far exceeds DeBERTa (49.51\\%), the combo yields $\\Delta \\leq +0.49$\\,pp (all $p = 1.0$). Aggregation gain shrinks with model capability: $+8.01$\\,pp (Mistral-7B) to $+0.86$\\,pp (Qwen3-14B thinking mode; Appendix~\\ref{app:single-trace}). Across all conditions at $w{=}3$, combo gain tracks the capability gap (Figure~\\ref{fig:capability-gap}): meaningful improvement emerges only when the verifier's standalone accuracy approaches or exceeds the generator's SC baseline.\\footnote{DeBERTa-v3-large (45.17\\% standalone) yields $+4.83$\\,pp on Mistral+PW ($w{=}5$, $p{=}0.11$), matching the predicted ${\\sim}+5$\\,pp (gap$=+5.84$); see Table~\\ref{tab:cross_nli_scale}.}",
          "+Cross-architecture verification succeeds when the verifier's standalone accuracy exceeds the generator's SC baseline. On ProofWriter, DeBERTa standalone (55.00\\%) exceeds Mistral-7B SC (39.33\\%), enabling $+10.67$\\,pp at $w{=}5$ ($p < 0.001$). On Qwen3-14B FOLIO, where SC (86.27\\%) far exceeds DeBERTa (49.51\\%), the combo yields $\\Delta \\leq +0.49$\\,pp (all $p = 1.0$). Aggregation gain shrinks with model capability: $+8.01$\\,pp (Mistral-7B) to $+0.86$\\,pp (Qwen3-14B thinking mode; Appendix~\\ref{app:single-trace}). Across all conditions at $w{=}3$, combo gain tracks the capability gap (Figure~\\ref{fig:capability-gap}): meaningful improvement emerges only when the verifier's standalone accuracy approaches or exceeds the generator's SC baseline.",

1	\enlargethispage{2\baselineskip}
2	\section{Discussion}
3	\label{sec:discussion}
4	
5	The Symbolic Mirage manifests through two channels: the \emph{direct} limits voting diversity ($K_{\text{eff}} < 2$), while the \emph{indirect} collapses MAX-SAT to vote counting (Eq.~\ref{eq:degenerate}). The bias ratio gradient ($4.97\times$ Mistral $> 3.93\times$ Qwen $> 2.88\times$ LLaMA) aligns with regime boundaries; thinking-mode degeneration ($\kappa = 0.91$, Qwen3) confirms this scales with capability. For practitioners, $C_0$ with SC accuracy predicts when augmentation will fail. The independence-accuracy trade-off instantiates the classic ensemble tension \citep{krogh1994neural, brown2005diversity}. If traces do not reflect actual reasoning \citep{turpin2024language}, the mirage is inherent to extraction. Our extensions of prior negative results \citep{huang2024selfcorrect, stechly2025selfverification}---Effective-$K$, bias ratio, and process-level similarity---provide reusable diagnostics for any trace-aggregation system.
6	
```

```json
{
  "type": "queue-operation",
  "operation": "enqueue",
  "timestamp": "2026-05-20T20:08:49.583Z",
  "sessionId": "d08365c0-89a0-49b9-b6fd-c6c2b90cc0a7",
  "content": "[审稿报告]\nOverall: 3.0 (Findings)\n\n# 审稿报告 — Round 8 (Fresh Review)\n\n**论文**: \"Beyond Self-Consistency: Why Self-Extracted Symbolic Constraints Do Not Improve LLM Reasoning\"\n**Venue**: EMNLP 2026 (ARR May 2026 cycle, deadline 2026-05-25)\n**Reviewers**: R1 (方法严谨性 + 实验设计), R2 (新颖性 + 完整性 + Related Work + 写作质量)\n\n---\n\n## Summary\n\n本文研究 LLM 自提取的符号约束能否通过 Symbolic Invariant Consensus Aggregation (SICA)——基于 FOL 约束提取和 Z3 weighted partial MAX-SAT——改善 Self-Consistency majority voting。在 6 个模型（7B–32B）、3 个推理域、3000+ 条件评估中，自提取约束未能显著提升准确率。作者将此归因于 \"Symbolic Mirage\"：自提取约束是模型分布偏差的投射（confirmation bias rate 83.3%, BR ≈ 1.97+），trace 之间的 process-level 相关性（cosine similarity 0.69）远超 answer-level 独立性（κ ≈ 0.41）。25+ 补救策略均失败，揭示持久的 independence-accuracy trade-off。Oracle controls 确认聚合机制 sound（+29.90pp, +14.22pp, +11.50pp），瓶颈在自提取质量。Cross-architecture DeBERTa NLI verifier 在 ProofWriter 上显示初步正向信号。\n\n---\n\n## Strengths\n\n**S1. 实验规模和系统性出色。** 6 模型 × 6 数据集 × 3 推理域覆盖高/中/低 SC 准确率区间，25+ 补救策略的穷举式搜索在负面结果论文中罕见，大幅增强结论可信度。[R1-S2, R1-S6, R2-S2]\n\n**S2. 统计方法论严谨。** TOST 等价性检验（±2pp margin）确认负面结果而非仅依赖 NHST，Bootstrap 95% CI（10,000 resamples）、BH 多重比较校正、统计功效分析均超出 NLP 论文平均水准。[R1-S4, R2-S1]\n\n**S3. Oracle controls 设计巧妙。** Gold FOL oracle 大幅提升性能（Mistral-7B FOLIO +29.90pp, Qwen-14B FOLIO +14.22pp, ProofWriter +11.50pp），精确分离聚合机制（sound）与提取质量（bottleneck）。[R1-S3, R2-S5]\n\n**S4. CJT 框架和量化诊断工具有独立贡献。** Effective-K（§5.1）、Bias Ratio（§3.3）、C_D diagnostic 将 SC 失效模式诊断从定性变为定量，可被后续工作直接复用。[R1-S1, R1-S5, R2-S3]\n\n**S5. Independence-Accuracy Trade-off（Figure 7）是最具洞察力的贡献。** 清晰展示同模型方法的 κ ≈ 0.40–0.47 ceiling，Debate 降 κ 但毁 accuracy，形成 Pareto 关系。[R2-S4]\n\n**S6. 叙事结构完整。** 假设建立 → 主实验否定 → 三重诊断 → 补救失败 → 跨架构出口 → 讨论，逻辑连贯。格式合规：8 页正文、Limitations 完整、双盲匿名、48 条引用。[R2-S6, R2-S7]\n\n---\n\n## Weaknesses\n\n### Must-Fix（影响接收决定）\n\n**W1. FOL 术语误导，负面结论 scope 不清晰。** [R1-N1 critical, R2 agrees at warning]\n论文通篇使用 \"first-order logic (FOL) constraints\"，但 Limitations（p.21）承认提取结果 \"predominantly propositional ground clauses without quantifiers\"。数学域 extraction prompt 提取的是 equation/inequality/comparison，无量词。标题使用 \"Symbolic Constraints\" 无误，但正文大量出现 \"FOL constraints\" 有术语膨胀。\n- **Action**: (1) 统一改为 \"symbolic/logical constraints\"，仅在 Z3 接口描述中保留 \"FOL-style\"；(2) 报告含量词约束的比例（按域/模型）；(3) Discussion 讨论真正 FOL 提取是否改变结论。\n- **R2 缓解观点**: 命题逻辑 ⊂ FOL，如果更弱形式都失败，更强形式失败可能性只会更大——但这需要在论文中显式论证，不能让读者自己推断。\n\n**W2. Cross-architecture PoC 双重 underpowered。** [R1-N2 critical + R2-W2 warning, 双方交叉确认]\n- **生成端**（R1）: 仅 Mistral-7B 一个生成模型，不同模型有 Type A/B/C 不同行为模式。\n- **验证端**（R2）: FOLIO +2.94pp (n.s.) vs. ProofWriter SICA 自身 −2.83pp (p=0.014)，两个数据集方向相反。α=3 在 n=204 上有 overfitting 风险。\n- **综合**: §6 \"confirming the effect is not verifier-specific\" 仅基于三个 NLI 架构在同一生成模型上的一致性，不等于跨生成模型一致性。\n- **Action**: 至少补一个 Qwen2.5-14B 的 DeBERTa combo 实验（traces 已有）；将 §6 结论 qualify 为 \"preliminary signal on Mistral-7B ProofWriter\"；讨论 α selection 的 overfitting 风险。\n\n### Should-Fix（提升论文质量）\n\n**W3. 增量新颖性定位需加强。** [R2-W1 warning, R1 partial REBUT — **DIVERGENCE**]\n- **R2 立场**: Huang et al. (2024) self-correction failure、Stechly et al. (2025) self-verification limitations、Amonkar et al. (2025) LLM formalization quality、Chan et al. (2026) logical soundness limitations——核心洞察在概念层面已被覆盖。Excitement 2.5/5。\n- **R1 立场**: 本文测试了一个更强的假设——FOL + Z3 确定性 solver 能否绕过自然语言 self-correction 的局限。BR/C_D/Eff-K 是新的量化工具，先行工作停留在 \"doesn't work\"，本文给出 \"why, how much, where exactly\"。\n- **共识**: §2 Related Work 应更明确 position：不是重复 \"self-correction fails\"，而是 \"即便引入确定性形式验证（FOL + Z3），self-extraction 的 confirmation bias 仍然阻断这条路径\"。\n\n**W4. Domain + Model 覆盖范围与 Abstract 表述不匹配。** [R1-W3 + R2-W4, 双方共识]\n- Abstract \"across the full LLM capability range\" 仅 7B–32B 开源模型。\n- \"Three reasoning domains\" 中数学域全部 degenerate（SC ≥ 85%），主要结论限于 formal/logical reasoning。\n- **Action**: Abstract 修改为 \"across a range of open-source LLMs (7B–32B)\" + \"formal and logical reasoning domains, with exploratory results on mathematical reasoning\"。\n\n**W5. Symbolic Confirmation Bias 缺乏与 Turpin et al. (2024) unfaithful CoT 的理论连接。** [R2-W3, R1 agrees]\nBR = 1.97 可能是 unfaithful CoT 在 FOL 层面的投射。论文未区分 (a) 模型故意生成支持错误答案的约束 vs. (b) 约束生成与答案生成共享同一 biased representation。\n- **Action**: §3.3 增加 Turpin et al. 连接讨论。\n\n**W6. 种子复制 + 统计功效标注不足。** [R1-W1 + R2-W5, 双方共识]\nTable 1 大多数条件未标注 seed 数量，7 个条件 n ≤ 60 MDE 极大，未区分 confirmatory vs. exploratory。\n- **R2 缓解观点**: TOST 用 per-item paired comparison，统计结论本身 well-powered。但 Table 1 标注仍需改善。\n- **Action**: Table 1 增加 seed 列和 powered/exploratory 标注。\n\n**W7. 语义去重缺失 ablation。** [R1-W2 + R2-Q5, 双方共识]\nSyntactic dedup 可能系统性放大 verbose traces 权重。论文未报告去重前后约束数量比，未做 Z3 semantic equivalence ablation。\n- **Action**: 报告去重统计；讨论影响或做 ablation。\n\n**W8. 约束质量分析仅覆盖单一模型。** [R1-W4, R2 agrees]\nExtraction error taxonomy (N=804) 仅 Qwen2.5-14B on FOLIO + ProofWriter。91.8% discriminative rate 在 Type B/C 模型上可能不同。\n- **Action**: 扩展到 2-3 个模型，或明确限定 claim scope。\n\n### Nice-to-Have\n\n**W9.** Figure 5（intervention strategies summary）深埋 Appendix p.22，应移入正文——两位 Reviewer + 视觉审查均建议。[R2-W6, R1 agrees]\n\n**W10.** Figure 2 线性回归 R² ≈ 4.4%，需说明 R² 并讨论离群点影响。[R1-W5]\n\n**W11.** 83.3% confirmation bias 是 aggregate 数字，per-model 变异大（69%–94%），主文应报告 breakdown。[R1-Q3]\n\n---\n\n## Reviewer Consensus\n\n以下问题双方达成共识（[AGREE]）：\n1. **PoC 不足**: Cross-architecture PoC 在生成端（single model）和验证端（inconsistent datasets）双重 underpowered [R1-N2 + R2-W2]\n2. **术语修正**: \"FOL constraints\" 应改为 \"symbolic/logical constraints\" [R1-N1 + R2 agrees]\n3. **覆盖范围校准**: Abstract 的 domain 和 model claims 需 downscope [R1-W3 + R2-W4]\n4. **去重 ablation**: Syntactic-only dedup 需量化影响 [R1-W2 + R2-Q5]\n5. **Table 1 标注**: 需区分 powered vs. exploratory conditions [R1-W1 + R2-W5]\n6. **Missing refs**: Weng et al. (2023) 和 Madaan et al. (2023) 应引用 [R1 + R2]\n7. **Figure 5 提升**: 应从 Appendix 移入正文 [R1 + R2 + Visual Check]\n8. **Turpin 连接**: §3.3 应与 unfaithful CoT 框架建立理论联系 [R2-W3, R1 agrees]\n\n## Reviewer Divergence\n\n**核心分歧：增量新颖性评估（W3）**\n- **R1 (3.5)**: FOL + Z3 确定性验证路径的失败是非平凡实证贡献；BR/C_D/Eff-K 量化工具在先行工作中无对应物；实证 specificity 超越了概念层面的 \"self-correction fails\"。\n- **R2 (3.0)**: 核心洞察——\"自提取符号结构继承模型偏差\"——在概念层面已被 Huang/Stechly/Amonkar/Chan 覆盖；具体化到 FOL + SC 组合的增量有限。Excitement 2.5/5。\n- **Director 评估**: 这是 legitimate disagreement。论文的实证严谨性毋庸置疑，但 excitement/novelty 是区分 Main Conference 和 Findings 的关键。如果 AC 认为量化诊断工具（BR, Eff-K）和 Z3 deterministic path 的 specific test 构成 sufficient novelty，3.5 可辩护；如果认为概念增量有限，3.0 更合适。\n\n---\n\n## Questions for Authors\n\n1. 含一阶量词（∀, ∃）约束的比例？按域和模型报告。[R1-Q1]\n2. 是否在 Qwen/LLaMA 上测试过 DeBERTa NLI combo？[R1-Q2]\n3. 83.3% confirmation bias 的 per-model breakdown（Table 13 显示 69%–94% 变异）？[R1-Q3]\n4. 去重前后平均约束数量和唯一约束率？[R1-Q4]\n5. Fine-tuned FOL extractor 能否显著提升 discriminative rate？[R2-Q1]\n6. Figure 7 independence-accuracy trade-off 是否有 CJT-based 理论下界？[R2-Q2]\n7. DeBERTa NLI 在 ProofWriter 上 same-model SICA degradation（−2.83pp）的根本原因？[R2-Q3]\n\n---\n\n## Missing References\n\n**需要引用**（双方共识）：\n- Weng et al. (2023) \"Large Language Models are Better Reasoners with Self-Verification\" — 正面 self-verification 结果，需在 §2 讨论为何不推广到 FOL 约束提取\n- Madaan et al. (2023) \"Self-Refine: Iterative Refinement with Self-Feedback\" — iterative self-improvement 代表工作，与 §5.4 remediation strategies 直接相关\n\n**可选**：\n- Kadavath et al. (2022) \"Language Models (Mostly) Know What They Know\" — R2 建议，R1 认为非必须（calibration ≠ constraint extraction）\n- Lightman et al. margin 溢出。**\n- **[info] 表格全部 booktabs 风格，排版规范。**\n\n---\n\n## Overall Assessment\n\n| 维度 | R1 | R2 | 综合 |\n|------|-----|-----|------|\n| Soundness | 3.5/5 | 4/5 | **3.75/5** — 方法论扎实，统计分析上乘；FOL 术语和 **3.5** (Borderline Conference) | **3.0** (Findings) | **3.0 (Findings)** |\n| Confidence | 4/5 | 4/5 | **High** |\n\n**综合评分：3.0 — Findings**\n\n**评分理由：** 这是一篇方法论严谨、实验规模出色的负面结果论文。Symbolic Mirage 概念、BR/Eff-K 量化诊断工具、25+ 策略的系统性搜索对社区有明确参考价值。Oracle controls 有效分离了机制和提取质量问题。\n\n限制接收为 Findings 而非 Main Conference 的主要因素是：(1) 核心洞察相对于已有 self-verification failure 文献（Huang 2024, Stechly 2025）的增量新颖性有限——即使 W1（术语统一 + 量词分析）、W2（补 Qwen 模型 PoC 实验）、W3（加强 §2 novelty positioning），即可 borderline conference。\n\n---\n\n## Detailed Comments\n\n### §1 Introduction\n- 动机清晰，CJT → independence bottleneck → symbolic extraction 逻辑链流畅\n- Contributions 3 点概括准确\n- \"FOL constraints\" 首次出现时应明确约束的实际 formalism 层级 [W1]\n\n### §2 Related Work\n- 覆盖充分，分 SC extensions、neuro-symbolic reasoning、test-time compute 三个子节\n- (2024) unfaithful CoT 建立理论连接 [W5]；C_D \"zero-valued\" 表述可更精确\n\n### §4 Experiments\n- Table 1 信息密度高，但需增加 seed 列和 powered/exploratory 标注 [W6]\n- Type A/B/C regime 分类有用\n- Oracle control 是论文最强 positive control\n\n### §5 Analysis\n- §5.1 Eff-K 是扎实的理论贡献\n- §5.2 83.3% BR 需报告 per-model breakdown（69%–94% 变异）[W11]\n- §5.3 TF-IDF cosine similarity 是合理 proxy 但应讨论局限\n- §5.4 25+ strategies Figure 5 应在正文 [W9]\n\n### §6 Cross-Architecture PoC\n- 方法设计合理（encoder-only NLI as external verifier）\n- 单生成模型 + 不一致数据集结果严重限制结论强度 [W2]\n- α selection 缺乏 principled justification，有 overfitting 风险\n\n### §7-8 Discussion & Conclusion\n- \"driver limits voting diversity\" vs. \"indirect collapse\" 双通道分析简洁有力\n- Figure 7 是论文最有洞察力的图之一——考虑移入正文\n- Conclusion 恰当总结负面发现和正向逃逸路线\n\n### Limitations\n- 诚实且全面\n- Extended Limitations (§Y) 与 main Limitations 有部分冗余\n\n### Appendices\n- 总体高质量，Appendix C (Z3), D (diagnostics), E (power analysis), G (case studies) 尤其有价值\n- 25 个 appendix 略多，部分可合并\n---\n\n## ⚠️ 处理协议(强制,逐条执行,禁止简略)\n\n**收到上面的审稿报告后必须按以下顺序处理**:\n\n1. **逐条编号通读**: 把报告里所有问题、修改建议一项一项列出来(R1, R2, R3...),\n   即使有的看起来重复或次要也要单独保留 — 不允许漏任何一条。\n\n2. **每条给出详细修复方案**:\n   - # 审稿报告 — Round 8 (Fresh Review)

**论文**: "Beyond Self-Consistency: Why Self-Extracted Symbolic Constraints Do Not Improve LLM Reasoning"
**Venue**: EMNLP 2026 (ARR May 2026 cycle, deadline 2026-05-25)
**Reviewers**: R1 (方法严谨性 + 实验设计), R2 (新颖性 + 完整性 + Related Work + 写作质量)

---

## Summary

本文研究 LLM 自提取的符号约束能否通过 Symbolic Invariant Consensus Aggregation (SICA)——基于 FOL 约束提取和 Z3 weighted partial MAX-SAT——改善 Self-Consistency majority voting。在 6 个模型（7B–32B）、3 个推理域、3000+ 条件评估中，自提取约束未能显著提升准确率。作者将此归因于 "Symbolic Mirage"：自提取约束是模型分布偏差的投射（confirmation bias rate 83.3%, BR ≈ 1.97+），trace 之间的 process-level 相关性（cosine similarity 0.69）远超 answer-level 独立性（κ ≈ 0.41）。25+ 补救策略均失败，揭示持久的 independence-accuracy trade-off。Oracle controls 确认聚合机制 sound（+29.90pp, +14.22pp, +11.50pp），瓶颈在自提取质量。Cross-architecture DeBERTa NLI verifier 在 ProofWriter 上显示初步正向信号。

---

## Strengths

**S1. 实验规模和系统性出色。** 6 模型 × 6 数据集 × 3 推理域覆盖高/中/低 SC 准确率区间，25+ 补救策略的穷举式搜索在负面结果论文中罕见，大幅增强结论可信度。[R1-S2, R1-S6, R2-S2]

**S2. 统计方法论严谨。** TOST 等价性检验（±2pp margin）确认负面结果而非仅依赖 NHST，Bootstrap 95% CI（10,000 resamples）、BH 多重比较校正、统计功效分析均超出 NLP 论文平均水准。[R1-S4, R2-S1]

**S3. Oracle controls 设计巧妙。** Gold FOL oracle 大幅提升性能（Mistral-7B FOLIO +29.90pp, Qwen-14B FOLIO +14.22pp, ProofWriter +11.50pp），精确分离聚合机制（sound）与提取质量（bottleneck）。[R1-S3, R2-S5]

**S4. CJT 框架和量化诊断工具有独立贡献。** Effective-K（§5.1）、Bias Ratio（§3.3）、C_D diagnostic 将 SC 失效模式诊断从定性变为定量，可被后续工作直接复用。[R1-S1, R1-S5, R2-S3]

**S5. Independence-Accuracy Trade-off（Figure 7）是最具洞察力的贡献。** 清晰展示同模型方法的 κ ≈ 0.40–0.47 ceiling，Debate 降 κ 但毁 accuracy，形成 Pareto 关系。[R2-S4]

**S6. 叙事结构完整。** 假设建立 → 主实验否定 → 三重诊断 → 补救失败 → 跨架构出口 → 讨论，逻辑连贯。格式合规：8 页正文、Limitations 完整、双盲匿名、48 条引用。[R2-S6, R2-S7]

---

## Weaknesses

### Must-Fix（影响接收决定）

**W1. FOL 术语误导，负面结论 scope 不清晰。** [R1-N1 critical, R2 agrees at warning]
论文通篇使用 "first-order logic (FOL) constraints"，但 Limitations（p.21）承认提取结果 "predominantly propositional ground clauses without quantifiers"。数学域 extraction prompt 提取的是 equation/inequality/comparison，无量词。标题使用 "Symbolic Constraints" 无误，但正文大量出现 "FOL constraints" 有术语膨胀。
- **Action**: (1) 统一改为 "symbolic/logical constraints"，仅在 Z3 接口描述中保留 "FOL-style"；(2) 报告含量词约束的比例（按域/模型）；(3) Discussion 讨论真正 FOL 提取是否改变结论。
- **R2 缓解观点**: 命题逻辑 ⊂ FOL，如果更弱形式都失败，更强形式失败可能性只会更大——但这需要在论文中显式论证，不能让读者自己推断。

**W2. Cross-architecture PoC 双重 underpowered。** [R1-N2 critical + R2-W2 warning, 双方交叉确认]
- **生成端**（R1）: 仅 Mistral-7B 一个生成模型，不同模型有 Type A/B/C 不同行为模式。
- **验证端**（R2）: FOLIO +2.94pp (n.s.) vs. ProofWriter SICA 自身 −2.83pp (p=0.014)，两个数据集方向相反。α=3 在 n=204 上有 overfitting 风险。
- **综合**: §6 "confirming the effect is not verifier-specific" 仅基于三个 NLI 架构在同一生成模型上的一致性，不等于跨生成模型一致性。
- **Action**: 至少补一个 Qwen2.5-14B 的 DeBERTa combo 实验（traces 已有）；将 §6 结论 qualify 为 "preliminary signal on Mistral-7B ProofWriter"；讨论 α selection 的 overfitting 风险。

### Should-Fix（提升论文质量）

**W3. 增量新颖性定位需加强。** [R2-W1 warning, R1 partial REBUT — **DIVERGENCE**]
- **R2 立场**: Huang et al. (2024) self-correction failure、Stechly et al. (2025) self-verification limitations、Amonkar et al. (2025) LLM formalization quality、Chan et al. (2026) logical soundness limitations——核心洞察在概念层面已被覆盖。Excitement 2.5/5。
- **R1 立场**: 本文测试了一个更强的假设——FOL + Z3 确定性 solver 能否绕过自然语言 self-correction 的局限。BR/C_D/Eff-K 是新的量化工具，先行工作停留在 "doesn't work"，本文给出 "why, how much, where exactly"。
- **共识**: §2 Related Work 应更明确 position：不是重复 "self-correction fails"，而是 "即便引入确定性形式验证（FOL + Z3），self-extraction 的 confirmation bias 仍然阻断这条路径"。

**W4. Domain + Model 覆盖范围与 Abstract 表述不匹配。** [R1-W3 + R2-W4, 双方共识]
- Abstract "across the full LLM capability range" 仅 7B–32B 开源模型。
- "Three reasoning domains" 中数学域全部 degenerate（SC ≥ 85%），主要结论限于 formal/logical reasoning。
- **Action**: Abstract 修改为 "across a range of open-source LLMs (7B–32B)" + "formal and logical reasoning domains, with exploratory results on mathematical reasoning"。

**W5. Symbolic Confirmation Bias 缺乏与 Turpin et al. (2024) unfaithful CoT 的理论连接。** [R2-W3, R1 agrees]
BR = 1.97 可能是 unfaithful CoT 在 FOL 层面的投射。论文未区分 (a) 模型故意生成支持错误答案的约束 vs. (b) 约束生成与答案生成共享同一 biased representation。
- **Action**: §3.3 增加 Turpin et al. 连接讨论。

**W6. 种子复制 + 统计功效标注不足。** [R1-W1 + R2-W5, 双方共识]
Table 1 大多数条件未标注 seed 数量，7 个条件 n ≤ 60 MDE 极大，未区分 confirmatory vs. exploratory。
- **R2 缓解观点**: TOST 用 per-item paired comparison，统计结论本身 well-powered。但 Table 1 标注仍需改善。
- **Action**: Table 1 增加 seed 列和 powered/exploratory 标注。

**W7. 语义去重缺失 ablation。** [R1-W2 + R2-Q5, 双方共识]
Syntactic dedup 可能系统性放大 verbose traces 权重。论文未报告去重前后约束数量比，未做 Z3 semantic equivalence ablation。
- **Action**: 报告去重统计；讨论影响或做 ablation。

**W8. 约束质量分析仅覆盖单一模型。** [R1-W4, R2 agrees]
Extraction error taxonomy (N=804) 仅 Qwen2.5-14B on FOLIO + ProofWriter。91.8% discriminative rate 在 Type B/C 模型上可能不同。
- **Action**: 扩展到 2-3 个模型，或明确限定 claim scope。

### Nice-to-Have

**W9.** Figure 5（intervention strategies summary）深埋 Appendix p.22，应移入正文——两位 Reviewer + 视觉审查均建议。[R2-W6, R1 agrees]

**W10.** Figure 2 线性回归 R² ≈ 4.4%，需说明 R² 并讨论离群点影响。[R1-W5]

**W11.** 83.3% confirmation bias 是 aggregate 数字，per-model 变异大（69%–94%），主文应报告 breakdown。[R1-Q3]

---

## Reviewer Consensus

以下问题双方达成共识（[AGREE]）：
1. **PoC 不足**: Cross-architecture PoC 在生成端（single model）和验证端（inconsistent datasets）双重 underpowered [R1-N2 + R2-W2]
2. **术语修正**: "FOL constraints" 应改为 "symbolic/logical constraints" [R1-N1 + R2 agrees]
3. **覆盖范围校准**: Abstract 的 domain 和 model claims 需 downscope [R1-W3 + R2-W4]
4. **去重 ablation**: Syntactic-only dedup 需量化影响 [R1-W2 + R2-Q5]
5. **Table 1 标注**: 需区分 powered vs. exploratory conditions [R1-W1 + R2-W5]
6. **Missing refs**: Weng et al. (2023) 和 Madaan et al. (2023) 应引用 [R1 + R2]
7. **Figure 5 提升**: 应从 Appendix 移入正文 [R1 + R2 + Visual Check]
8. **Turpin 连接**: §3.3 应与 unfaithful CoT 框架建立理论联系 [R2-W3, R1 agrees]

## Reviewer Divergence

**核心分歧：增量新颖性评估（W3）**
- **R1 (3.5)**: FOL + Z3 确定性验证路径的失败是非平凡实证贡献；BR/C_D/Eff-K 量化工具在先行工作中无对应物；实证 specificity 超越了概念层面的 "self-correction fails"。
- **R2 (3.0)**: 核心洞察——"自提取符号结构继承模型偏差"——在概念层面已被 Huang/Stechly/Amonkar/Chan 覆盖；具体化到 FOL + SC 组合的增量有限。Excitement 2.5/5。
- **Director 评估**: 这是 legitimate disagreement。论文的实证严谨性毋庸置疑，但 excitement/novelty 是区分 Main Conference 和 Findings 的关键。如果 AC 认为量化诊断工具（BR, Eff-K）和 Z3 deterministic path 的 specific test 构成 sufficient novelty，3.5 可辩护；如果认为概念增量有限，3.0 更合适。

---

## Questions for Authors

1. 含一阶量词（∀, ∃）约束的比例？按域和模型报告。[R1-Q1]
2. 是否在 Qwen/LLaMA 上测试过 DeBERTa NLI combo？[R1-Q2]
3. 83.3% confirmation bias 的 per-model breakdown（Table 13 显示 69%–94% 变异）？[R1-Q3]
4. 去重前后平均约束数量和唯一约束率？[R1-Q4]
5. Fine-tuned FOL extractor 能否显著提升 discriminative rate？[R2-Q1]
6. Figure 7 independence-accuracy trade-off 是否有 CJT-based 理论下界？[R2-Q2]
7. DeBERTa NLI 在 ProofWriter 上 same-model SICA degradation（−2.83pp）的根本原因？[R2-Q3]

## Missing References

**需要引用**（双方共识）：
- Weng et al. (2023) "Large Language Models are Better Reasoners with Self-Verification" — 正面 self-verification 结果，需在 §2 讨论为何不推广到 FOL 约束提取
- Madaan et al. (2023) "Self-Refine: Iterative Refinement with Self-Feedback" — iterative self-improvement 代表工作，与 §5.4 remediation strategies 直接相关

**可选**：

- Lightman et al. (2024) "Let's Verify Step by Step" — R1 建议，但 R2 指出论文已引用（bib key: lightman2024prm）

## Overall Assessment

| 维度 | R1 | R2 | 综合 |
|------|-----|-----|------|
| Soundness | 3.5/5 | 4/5 | **3.75/5** — 方法论扎实，统计分析上乘；FOL 术语和 dedup 问题降低 claim 精度 |
| Excitement | 3/5 | 2.5/5 | **2.75/5** — CJT 框架和 25+ 策略扫描有价值，但核心洞察增量新颖性有争议 |
| Reproducibility | 4/5 | 4/5 | **4/5** — 详细 prompts、伪代码、参数报告；承诺代码公开；种子复制不一致扣分 |
| **Overall** | **3.5** (Borderline Conference) | **3.0** (Findings) | **3.0 (Findings)** |
| Confidence | 4/5 | 4/5 | **High** |

**综合评分：3.0 — Findings**

**评分理由：** 这是一篇方法论严谨、实验规模出色的负面结果论文。Symbolic Mirage 概念、BR/Eff-K 量化诊断工具、25+ 策略的系统性搜索对社区有明确参考价值。Oracle controls 有效分离了机制和提取质量问题。

限制接收为 Findings 而非 Main Conference 的主要因素是：(1) 核心洞察相对于已有 self-verification failure 文献（Huang 2024, Stechly 2025）的增量新颖性有限——即使 R1 的 rebuttal 部分成立（Z3 deterministic path 是 specific contribution），概念层面的 overlap 仍然存在；(2) 唯一的正向贡献（cross-architecture PoC）在生成端和验证端双重 underpowered；(3) FOL 术语偏差使负面结论的 scope 不够清晰。

**提升至 3.5 的路径：** 解决 W1（术语统一 + 量词分析）、W2（补 Qwen 模型 PoC 实验）、W3（加强 §2 novelty positioning），即可 borderline conference。

---

## Detailed Comments

### §1 Introduction
- 动机清晰，CJT → independence bottleneck → symbolic extraction 逻辑链流畅
- Contributions 3 点概括准确
- "FOL constraints" 首次出现时应明确约束的实际 formalism 层级 [W1]

### §2 Related Work
- 覆盖充分，分 SC extensions、neuro-symbolic reasoning、test-time compute 三个子节
- **缺**: Weng et al. (2023) positive self-verification 和 Madaan et al. (2023) Self-Refine 未引用
- **缺**: 应更显式地 position 本文 vs. Huang/Stechly：不是 "self-correction fails again" 而是 "even deterministic formal verification fails when extraction is self-sourced" [W3]

### §3 Background & Setup
- §3.1 CJT 框架严谨，K_eff 公式引用正确
- §3.2 SICA pipeline 描述清楚
- §3.3 Symbolic Mirage 和 BR 定义清晰；但应与 Turpin et al. (2024) unfaithful CoT 建立理论连接 [W5]；C_D "zero-valued" 表述可更精确

### §4 Experiments
- Table 1 信息密度高，但需增加 seed 列和 powered/exploratory 标注 [W6]
- Type A/B/C regime 分类有用
- Oracle control 是论文最强 positive control

### §5 Analysis
- §5.1 Eff-K 是扎实的理论贡献
- §5.2 83.3% BR 需报告 per-model breakdown（69%–94% 变异）[W11]
- §5.3 TF-IDF cosine similarity 是合理 proxy 但应讨论局限
- §5.4 25+ strategies Figure 5 应在正文 [W9]

### §6 Cross-Architecture PoC
- 方法设计合理（encoder-only NLI as external verifier）
- 单生成模型 + 不一致数据集结果严重限制结论强度 [W2]
- α selection 缺乏 principled justification，有 overfitting 风险

### §7-8 Discussion & Conclusion
- "driver limits voting diversity" vs. "indirect collapse" 双通道分析简洁有力
- Figure 7 是论文最有洞察力的图之一——考虑移入正文
- Conclusion 恰当总结负面发现和正向逃逸路线

### Limitations
- 诚实且全面
- Extended Limitations (§Y) 与 main Limitations 有部分冗余

### Appendices
- 总体高质量，Appendix C (Z3), D (diagnostics), E (power analysis), G (case studies) 尤其有价值
- 25 个 appendix 略多，部分可合并

## ⚠️ 处理协议(强制,逐条执行,禁止简略)

**收到上面的审稿报告后必须按以下顺序处理**:

1. **逐条编号通读**: 把报告里所有问题、修改建议一项一项列出来(R1, R2, R3...),
   即使有的看起来重复或次要也要单独保留 — 不允许漏任何一条。

2. **每条给出详细修复方案**:
   - 补实验: 写明实验配置(baseline / 超参 / seed / dataset / metric) + 预估 GPU 时长
   - 改论文: 写明改哪一节、改写思路、新引用是否需要补
   - 改 framing / 措辞: 写明原文 + 改写后版本
   - 写 rebuttal: 写明论点和支撑证据
   - 拒绝: 写明拒绝理由(reviewer 误读 / 超出 scope / 已有反驳证据)

3. **必须调 `post_to_reviewer` 让 Research Reviewer 补充意见**:
   把完整审稿报告 + 你的逐条初步方案打包发给 Reviewer, 让 ta 独立审视有没有
   遗漏、方案可行性、技术细节是否合理。**这一步不可省** — Reviewer 是你的
   独立审查员, 他对每条审稿意见的判断和你互补。

4. **收到 Reviewer 补充后综合定稿**: 把 reviewer 反馈合并进你的 Action Matrix,
   再安排 Main Agent 执行(补实验 / 改论文)或向用户确认 venue 变更。

5. **同步 Issue Tracker**: Action Matrix 定稿后, 用 `read_local_file` 读
   `projects/{project}/artifacts/review_issues.yaml`, 把本轮处理的 issue 标
   `in_progress`、Reviewer 已确认修复的标 `resolved`, 调 `update_review_issues`
   写回。这是跨轮次的修复追踪 — Continue 模式审稿要靠这个表识别复发问题。

**禁止**:
- 跳过第 3 步直接派 Main Agent
- 把多条审稿意见合并成一条 action
- 对低 priority 意见用"暂不处理"一笔带过而不给出明确理由
- 整体性"已知悉"回复

_source line 4655 · 2026-05-20T20:09:10.293Z_

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01C9nz9nYYHXdeK4smNvaFjU`</summary>

```text
1	---
2	name: review-report-action
3	description: '当 Research Director 收到 Review 阶段回传的审稿报告时,立即使用本 skill。先做 Holistic Pass(识别 SYSTEMIC_ISSUE),再做 Per-Comment Action Matrix。禁止"已知悉"整体回复。'
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	---
8	
9	# Review Report Action Matrix
10	
11	## 硬约束
12	收到审稿报告后**禁止**整体性 "已知悉" 回复。必须**先做 Holistic Pass,再做 Per-Comment Action Matrix**。
13	
14	## Step 1: Holistic Pass（整体审视，action matrix 之前必做）
15	
16	审稿意见不是独立的,经常多条指向同一个系统性问题。切碎成多条独立 action 会漏掉根因。
17	
18	1. **聚类**：把所有审稿意见按主题聚类（method design / experiment design / claim strength / writing clarity / novelty / related work）
19	2. **识别系统性问题**：任何一个 cluster 含 ≥ 3 条意见 → 标记为 `[SYSTEMIC_ISSUE]`,不再切碎成独立 action,作为单独根因处理
20	3. **矩阵回溯**：如果 SYSTEMIC_ISSUE 指向 core method design → 立即触发 `experiment-matrix` skill 回顾:
21	   - 现有实验矩阵在新的 method 理解下还有几格有效？
22	   - 哪些之前的 positive 实验可能因此失效（method 基础变了）？
23	   - 哪些格子要重新跑？
24	4. **全局影响评估**：SYSTEMIC_ISSUE 是否影响论文 core claim？如果是 → 必须子调 `red-section` 做 framing 评估,然后再动 action matrix
25	
26	**Holistic Pass 输出写入 ROADMAP.md 的"审稿回溯·系统性问题"section**,先于 action matrix。
27	
28	## Step 2: Action Matrix 格式
29	
30	| 审稿意见 ID | 内容摘要 | 类别 | 动作 | 负责人 | ETA |
31	|-----------|--------|------|------|-------|-----|
32	| R1.1 | 缺某 ablation | 补实验 | 让 Agent 铺 exp_XXX | Agent | 2d |
33	| R1.2 | Claim 过强 | 改 paper | 调 red-section 重写 intro | Agent | 1d |
34	| R1.3 | 某 method 解释不清 | 写 rebuttal | Director 写 rebuttal 论点 | Director | 1d |
35	| R2.1 | Core claim 不成立 | framing 调整 | 子调 red-section + 改矩阵 | Agent + Director | 5d |
36	
37	## 类别
38	- **补实验**：必须让 Agent 铺实验（可与 rebuttal 并行）
39	- **改 paper**：让 Agent 调 red-section 后改章节
40	- **写 rebuttal**：Director 主笔
41	- **拒绝**：有理由的拒绝（写入 action matrix 的"拒绝理由"字段）
42	
43	## 产出
44	- action matrix 写入 ROADMAP.md 的"审稿回溯"section
45	- 决策日志同步（`log_decision`）
46	- 补实验条目立即调度（不等 rebuttal 写完）
47	- 如果有 "framing 调整" 类别 → 子调 red-section skill
48	
49	## Step 3: 同步 Issue Tracker（必做）
50	
51	action matrix 定稿后,同步更新跨轮次 Issue Tracker `artifacts/review_issues.yaml`:
52	
53	1. 用 `read_local_file("projects/{project}/artifacts/review_issues.yaml")` 读当前 tracker（不存在则视为空）
54	2. 对 action matrix 中每条:
55	   - 找到对应的 issue ID（同一 cluster / 相似 summary）
56	   - 把 `status` 从 `open` 翻到 `in_progress`
57	   - 在 `action_items` 里追加本轮 action（含 round / action / assigned_to / status）
58	3. 对上轮标 `in_progress` 且本轮 Reviewer 已确认修复（`[RESOLVED]`）的:
59	   - `status` 改为 `resolved`,记 `resolution_round` 和 `resolution`
60	4. 调 `update_review_issues(project, issues_yaml=完整 YAML)` 写回
61	
62	## 提交下一轮审稿（如适用）
63	
64	调 `submit_paper_to_review` 时根据情景设 `recommended_review_mode`:
65	
66	- **`continue`**：刚迭代修了上轮问题,需要 Reviewer 带着上轮记忆验证修复效果
67	- **`fresh`**：做了重大 reframing / 距上次审稿 >48h / 论文结构大改 → 想要不受干扰的客观评估
68	
69	同时在 `mode_reason` 写一句推荐理由。人类仍可在审批卡片上覆盖。
70	
```

director\n---\n\n# Review Report Action Matrix\n\n## 硬约束\n收到审稿报告后**禁止**整体性 \"已知悉\" 回复。必须**先做 Holistic Pass,再做 Per-Comment Action Matrix**。\n\n## Step 1: Holistic Pass（整体审视，action matrix 之前必做）\n\n审稿意见不是独立的,经常多条指向同一个系统性问题。切碎成多条独立 action 会漏掉根因。\n\n1. **聚类**：把所有审稿意见按主题聚类（method design / experiment design / claim strength / writing clarity / novelty / related work）\n2. **识别系统性问题**：任何一个 cluster 含 ≥ 3 条意见 → 标记为 `[SYSTEMIC_ISSUE]`,不再切碎成独立 action,作为单独根因处理\n3. **矩阵回溯**：如果 SYSTEMIC_ISSUE 指向 core method design → 立即触发 `experiment-matrix` skill 回顾:\n   - 现有实验矩阵在新的 method 理解下还有几格有效？\n   - 哪些之前的 positive 实验可能因此失效（method 基础变了）？\n   - 哪些格子要重新跑？\n4. **全局影响评估**：SYSTEMIC_ISSUE 是否影响论文 core claim？如果是 → 必须子调 `red-section` 做 framing 评估,然后再动 action matrix\n\n**Holistic Pass 输出写入 ROADMAP.md 的\"审稿回溯·系统性问题\"section**,先于 action matrix。\n\n## Step 2: Action Matrix 格式\n\n| 审稿意见 ID | 内容摘要 | 类别 | 动作 | 负责人 | ETA |\n|-----------|--------|------|------|-------|-----|\n| R1.1 | 缺某 ablation | 补实验 | 让 Agent 铺 exp_XXX | Agent | 2d |\n| R1.2 | Claim 过强 | 改 paper | 调 red-section 重写 intro | Agent | 1d |\n| R1.3 | 某 method 解释不清 | 写 rebuttal | Director 写 rebuttal 论点 | Director | 1d |\n| R2.1 | Core claim 不成立 | framing 调整 | 子调 red-section + 改矩阵 | Agent + Director | 5d |\n\n## 类别\n- **补实验**：必须让 Agent 铺实验（可与 rebuttal 并行）\n- **改 paper**：让 Agent 调 red-section 后改章节\n- **写 rebuttal**：Director 主笔\n- **拒绝**：有理由的拒绝（写入 action matrix 的\"拒绝理由\"字段）\n\n## 产出\n- action matrix 写入 ROADMAP.md 的\"审稿回溯\"section\n- 决策日志同步（`log_decision`）\n- 补实验条目立即调度（不等 rebuttal 写完）\n- 如果有 \"framing 调整\" 类别 → 子调 red-section skill\n\n## Step 3: 同步 Issue Tracker（必做）\n\naction matrix 定稿后,同步更新跨轮次 Issue Tracker `artifacts/review_issues.yaml`:\n\n1. 用 `read_local_file(\"projects/{project}/artifacts/review_issues.yaml\")` 读当前 tracker（不存在则视为空）\n2. 对 action matrix 中每条:\n   - 找到对应的 issue ID（同一 cluster / 相似 summary）\n   - 把 `status` 从 `open` 翻到 `in_progress`\n   - 在 `action_items` 里追加本轮 action（含 round / action / assigned_to / status）\n3. 对上轮标 `in_progress` 且本轮 Reviewer 已确认修复（`[RESOLVED]`）的:\n   - `status` 改为 `resolved`,记 `resolution_round` 和 `resolution`\n4. 调 `update_review_issues(project, issues_yaml=完整 YAML)` 写回\n\n## 提交下一轮审稿（如适用）\n\n调 `submit_paper_to_review` 时根据情景设 `recommended_review_mode`:\n\n- **`continue`**：刚迭代修了上轮问题,需要 Reviewer 带着上轮记忆验证修复效果\n- **`fresh`**：做了重大 reframing / 距上次审稿 >48h / 论文结构大改 → 想要不受干扰的客观评估\n\n同时在 `mode_reason` 写一句推荐理由。人类仍可在审批卡片上覆盖。\n",
      "numLines": 70,

      "totalLines": 70
    }

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **SICA**: Symbolic Invariant Consensus Aggregation — FOL constraint extraction + MAX-SAT via Z3
   - **Symbolic Mirage Hypothesis**: Self-extracted constraints are bias projections, not independent verification. BR(M,T) quantifies this. Three testable predictions (LOCKED).
   - **Bias Ratio**: BR = P(c supports a_M | a_M ≠ a*) / P(c supports a* | a_M ≠ a*). Mistral-7B: 4.97×, Qwen-14B: 3.93×, LLaMA: 2.88×
   - **Effective-K**: K/(1+(K-1)κ). Ranges 1.20-3.62 for K=12.
   - **Cross-Architecture PoC**: DeBERTa-large-MNLI (406M) + Mistral-7B SC. ProofWriter w=3: +3.50pp (p=0.013, BH adj p=0.038). w=5: +10.67pp (p<0.001).
   - **Multi-verifier confirmation (Dir-J3)**: DeBERTa, RoBERTa, BART all show significant gains on ProofWriter at w≥3
   - **Capability gap hypothesis**: NLI escape works only when (1) task-verifier alignment (LogiQA fails) AND (2) verifier standalone > SC baseline (strong models fail)
   - **NLI Matrix pattern**: FOLIO Mistral w=3 +2.94pp n.s.; PW Mistral w=3 +3.50pp significant; PW Qwen3 w=3 +0.50pp n.s.; FOLIO Qwen3 w=3 +0.49pp n.s.; LogiQA Mistral w=3 +0.00pp n.s.
   - **Dir-I HGE**: CONFIRMED NEGATIVE — Qwen3-14B standalone 83.82% > HGE-all 82.35%. Gain from capability mixing, not independence.
   - **Dir-I3**: Qwen3-14B thinking SICA: +0.49pp (p=1.0, 3 discordant pairs). SC=86.27% (176/204).

        "content": "\\section{Discussion}\n\\label{sec:discussion}\n\nThe Symbolic Mirage manifests through two channels: the \\emph{direct} limits voting diversity ($K_{\\text{eff}} < 2$), while the \\emph{indirect} collapses MAX-SAT to vote counting (Eq.~\\ref{eq:degenerate}). The bias ratio gradient ($4.97\\times$ Mistral $> 3.93\\times$ Qwen $> 2.88\\times$ LLaMA) aligns with regime boundaries; thinking-mode degeneration ($\\kappa = 0.91$, Qwen3) confirms this scales with capability. For practitioners, $C_0$ with SC accuracy predicts when augmentation will fail. The independence-accuracy trade-off instantiates the classic ensemble tension \\citep{krogh1994neural, brown2005diversity}. If traces do not reflect actual reasoning \\citep{turpin2024language}, the mirage is inherent to extraction. Our extensions of prior negative results \\citep{huang2024selfcorrect, stechly2025selfverification}---Effective-$K$, bias ratio, and process-level similarity---provide reusable diagnostics for any trace-aggregation system.\n",
        "numLines": 5,
        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 5
      }

    "content": {
      "type": "text",
      "file": {
        "filePath": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_3/projects/symb_invariant_consensus/docs/paper/solutions.tex",
        "content": "\\section{Cross-Architecture Proof-of-Concept}\n\\label{sec:solutions}\n\\label{sec:cross-arch}\n\nThe analyses in \\S\\ref{sec:confirmation-bias}--\\S\\ref{sec:remediation} confirm all three predictions of the Symbolic Mirage hypothesis (\\S\\ref{sec:mirage}): self-extracted constraints inherit the generating model's bias. The oracle gap (\\S\\ref{sec:oracle}) confirms the mechanism is sound given uncontaminated constraints; the corrective signal must come from outside the model's distribution.\n\n\\paragraph{Method.}\nWe test this with DeBERTa-large \\citep{he2021deberta} (406M, encoder-only), fine-tuned on MultiNLI \\citep{williams2018mnli}, classifying (premise, conclusion) pairs into \\textsc{entailment}, \\textsc{contradiction}, or \\textsc{neutral}. In combo mode, the NLI verdict is integrated with SC's $K$ votes via weight $w$: each NLI-supporting vote counts $w$ times. We evaluate on Mistral-7B FOLIO ($n = 204$) and ProofWriter depth-5 ($n = 600$), because NLI classification requires (premise, conclusion) pairs that these datasets naturally provide; multiple-choice datasets (LogiQA) lack this structure.\n\n\\paragraph{Main result.}\nDeBERTa NLI alone achieves 49.51\\% on FOLIO (below SC's 55.88\\%) and 55.00\\% on ProofWriter (above SC's 39.33\\%). On FOLIO ($n = 204$), combo mode yields $+1.96$\\,pp at $w{=}1$ and $+2.94$\\,pp at $w{=}3$ (both n.s.). On ProofWriter ($n = 600$), $w{=}3$ achieves $+3.50$\\,pp ($p = 0.013$) and $w{=}5$ reaches $+10.67$\\,pp ($p < 0.001$)---while same-model SICA \\emph{degrades} ProofWriter ($-2.83$\\,pp, $p = 0.014$; Table~\\ref{tab:main_results}), illustrating that corrective signal must originate outside the model's distribution (\\S\\ref{sec:mirage}). We report $w{=}3$ as primary; higher weights risk overfitting. After Benjamini--Hochberg correction ($m{=}6$), ProofWriter $w{=}3$ remains significant (adjusted $p = 0.038$) and $w{=}5$ ($p < 0.001$); FOLIO comparisons are non-significant. Per-label caveat: DeBERTa over-predicts \\textsc{False} on ProofWriter (67.8\\%) and performs poorly on \\textsc{Unknown} (3.5\\% accuracy), as NLI's ``neutral'' fails to capture logical unknowability.\n\n\\paragraph{Why it works.}\nThe 406M encoder-only DeBERTa and 7B decoder-only Mistral share no architecture, training data, or inference mechanism, satisfying the independence condition that same-model sampling cannot provide (\\S\\ref{sec:mirage}). This produces per-label complementarity: NLI excels on \\textsc{False} problems (71.43\\% vs.\\ SC's 38.10\\%) where the LLM is weakest, while SC dominates \\textsc{Unknown} (68.12\\% vs.\\ 18.84\\%). All three NLI architectures tested---DeBERTa, RoBERTa, and BART (all MNLI-trained)---produce significant improvements on ProofWriter at $w \\geq 3$ (Appendix~\\ref{app:multi-nli}), confirming the effect is not verifier-specific.\n\n\\paragraph{Capability gap hypothesis.}\nCross-architecture verification succeeds when the verifier's standalone accuracy exceeds the generator's SC baseline. On ProofWriter, DeBERTa standalone (55.00\\%) exceeds Mistral-7B SC (39.33\\%), enabling $+10.67$\\,pp at $w{=}5$ ($p < 0.001$). On Qwen3-14B FOLIO, where SC (86.27\\%) far exceeds DeBERTa (49.51\\%), the combo yields $\\Delta \\leq +0.49$\\,pp (all $p = 1.0$). Aggregation gain shrinks with model capability: $+8.01$\\,pp (Mistral-7B) to $+0.86$\\,pp (Qwen3-14B thinking mode; Appendix~\\ref{app:single-trace}). Across all conditions at $w{=}3$, combo gain tracks the capability gap (Figure~\\ref{fig:capability-gap}): meaningful improvement emerges only when the verifier's standalone accuracy approaches or exceeds the generator's SC baseline.\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=0.82\\columnwidth]{figures/capability_gap_scatter}\n\\vspace{-3mm}\n\\caption{Combo gain vs.\\ capability gap (verifier standalone $-$ generator SC) at $w{=}3$. Meaningful gain emerges when the gap is positive (verifier exceeds generator).}\n\\label{fig:capability-gap}\n\\vspace{-6mm}\n\\end{figure}\n",
        "numLines": 27,
        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 27
      }

{
  "parentUuid": "50153318-453b-4dfe-9643-4146a4db1daa",
  "isSidechain": false,
  "attachment": {
    "type": "file",
    "filename": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_3/projects/symb_invariant_consensus/docs/paper/introduction.tex",
    "content": {
      "type": "text",
      "file": {
        "filePath": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_3/projects/symb_invariant_consensus/docs/paper/introduction.tex",
        "content": "\\section{Introduction}\\label{sec:introduction}\n\nTest-time computation scaling through multiple reasoning samples has emerged as a practical alternative to scaling model parameters \\citep{snell2025scaling, guo2025deepseekr1}.\nThe dominant aggregation strategy, Self-Consistency (SC) \\citep{wang2023sc}, samples $K$ chain-of-thought traces and selects the majority answer.\nSC is simple, model-agnostic, and effective---but it is hitting a ceiling.\n\\citet{loo2025reevaluating} demonstrate that gains from increasing $K$ plateau and even decline for strong models.\nThe gap between SC's theoretical promise (independent correct voters converge to truth) and its practical behavior (saturating gains) points to a structural mismatch: same-model traces carry correlated errors, and majority voting has no mechanism to correct shared bias.\n\nConfidence-weighted \\citep{taubenfeld2025cisc} and universal \\citep{chen2024usc} extensions alleviate this, but the core limitation persists.\nRecent neuro-symbolic methods \\citep{pan2023logiclm, olausson2023linc, xu2026dylos, li2025aristotle, wang2025fover, luo2025logicagent} report gains on logical reasoning but introduce components beyond the reasoning model.\nA simpler question precedes these: can the \\emph{same} model extract useful formal constraints from its own traces---a zero-cost upgrade requiring no additional model or data?\nNo prior work has systematically evaluated this.\n\nWe ground our analysis in the Condorcet Jury Theorem (CJT) \\citep{condorcet1785}: if $K$ independent voters each answer correctly with probability $p > 0.5$, the majority converges to truth \\citep{austensmith1996}.\nWhen independence fails \\citep{ladha1992correlated}, additional samples carry diminishing information.\nMeasuring inter-trace agreement with Fleiss' $\\kappa$ \\citep{fleiss1971kappa}, we find $\\kappa = 0.21$--$0.82$ across instruction-tuned conditions, yielding Effective-$K = 1.20$--$3.62$ for nominal $K = 12$: traces beyond a dozen add negligible independent information (\\S\\ref{sec:effective-k}).\nThe bottleneck runs deeper than answer-level agreement suggests.\nChain-of-thought cosine similarity ($0.69$) far exceeds answer-level $\\kappa$ ($0.41$), revealing that traces share reasoning processes even when they diverge on final answers (\\S\\ref{sec:process-kappa}).\nWhen the same model extracts FOL constraints from these correlated traces, the constraints exhibit \\emph{symbolic confirmation bias}: on problems where the model errs, 83.3\\% of self-extracted constraints support the wrong majority answer (bias ratio $4.97\\times$), and 94\\% of errors overlap with SC (\\S\\ref{sec:confirmation-bias}).\nSelf-extraction cannot supply the independent signal that CJT requires.\n\nWe study this through Symbolic Invariant Consensus Aggregation (SICA): extract FOL constraints from each of $K$ reasoning traces, deduplicate by syntactic equivalence, and select the answer best supported by a weighted partial MAX-SAT solution via Z3 \\citep{demoura2008z3} (\\S\\ref{sec:pipeline}; Figure~\\ref{fig:pipeline}).\nWe evaluate SICA across six models (7B--32B), three reasoning domains (FOLIO \\citep{han2022folio}, ProofWriter \\citep{tafjord2021proofwriter}, LogiQA \\citep{liu2020logiqa}), and over 3,000 condition-evaluations (${\\sim}$1,250 unique instances) with four-seed replication (Table~\\ref{tab:main_results}).\nTwenty-plus remediation strategies spanning fifteen families---from prompt engineering through method-level redesign---all produce neutral or negative results, confirming the bottleneck is persistent at the 7B--32B scale.\nWhile prior work qualitatively concludes that LLMs cannot self-correct or self-verify without external feedback \\citep{huang2024selfcorrect, stechly2025selfverification}, we contribute reusable quantitative diagnostics (Effective-$K$, bias ratio), show that formal Z3-checked verification also fails, and reveal process-level reasoning homogeneity invisible to answer-only metrics.\n\nOracle controls confirm the aggregation mechanism is sound: same-model gold FOL yields $+29.90$\\,pp (Mistral-7B) and $+14.22$\\,pp (Qwen-14B) on FOLIO ($p < 0.001$), yet self-extraction captures only ${\\sim}$12\\% of the same-model ceiling (\\S\\ref{sec:oracle}).\nThe central finding is a persistent independence-accuracy trade-off: Debate reduces $\\kappa$ from 0.44 to 0.15 (tripling Eff-$K$) but collapses accuracy by $-18.6$\\,pp; SC, Diverse-Prompt, and ICG all cluster at $\\kappa \\approx 0.40$--$0.47$, forming a same-model ceiling that holds across three model families (\\S\\ref{sec:remediation}).\nA cross-architecture proof-of-concept suggests this gap is recoverable: DeBERTa-large NLI combined with SC yields $+3.50$\\,pp on ProofWriter ($p = 0.013$; $+10.67$\\,pp at $w{=}5$, $p < 0.001$), confirmed across three NLI architectures (\\S\\ref{sec:cross-arch}).\n\nOur contributions are:\n\\begin{enumerate}[nosep,leftmargin=*]\n\\item \\textbf{Independence-Accuracy Trade-off.} Same-model $\\kappa \\geq 0.40$ across four methods and three model families; twenty-plus remediation strategies (fifteen families) fail. A cross-architecture NLI verifier ($+3.50$\\,pp on ProofWriter, $p = 0.013$; confirmed across three NLI architectures) suggests the ceiling is escapable (\\S\\ref{sec:remediation}, \\S\\ref{sec:cross-arch}).\n\n\\item \\textbf{Symbolic Confirmation Bias.} On incorrect problems, 83.3\\% of self-extracted constraints support the wrong majority answer (bias ratio $4.97\\times$), and 94\\% of errors overlap with SC (\\S\\ref{sec:confirmation-bias}).\n\n\\item \\textbf{Process-Level Independence.} Across four model families, CoT cosine similarity ($0.69$--$0.79$) far exceeds answer-level $\\kappa$ ($0.45$--$0.74$); the gap intensifies with model capability, showing reasoning homogeneity is architecture-independent (\\S\\ref{sec:process-kappa}).\n\\end{enumerate}\n",
        "numLines": 39,
        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 39
      }
    },
    "displayPath": "../../../docs/paper/introduction.tex"
  },

    "type": "file",

    "content": {
      "type": "text",
      "file": {
        "filePath": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_3/projects/symb_invariant_consensus/docs/paper/abstract.tex",
        "content": "Self-Consistency (SC) majority voting over chain-of-thought traces saturates as sample count grows because same-model traces are correlated voters.\nWe investigate whether symbolic constraint extraction can break this plateau through Symbolic Invariant Consensus Aggregation (SICA), which extracts first-order logic (FOL) constraints from reasoning traces and selects answers via weighted partial MAX-SAT.\nAcross the full LLM capability range---from Mistral-7B (54\\% SC) to Qwen3-14B thinking mode (86\\% SC)---and three reasoning domains with over 3,000 condition-evaluations (${\\sim}$1,250 unique instances), we find that self-extracted constraints do not improve majority voting at any capacity level: none of sixteen non-degenerate conditions shows a significant positive effect; TOST equivalence tests (margin $\\pm 2$\\,pp) establish equivalence for four conditions, while five show genuine SICA--SC differences---four of which are negative.\nWe identify \\emph{symbolic confirmation bias} as the root cause: on incorrect problems, 83.3\\% of self-extracted constraints support the wrong majority answer (bias ratio $4.97\\times$), and 94\\% of errors overlap with SC.\nProcess-level analysis reveals a deeper bottleneck: chain-of-thought cosine similarity ($0.69$) far exceeds answer-level Fleiss' $\\kappa$ ($0.41$), indicating that traces share reasoning processes even when they diverge on answers.\nTwenty-plus same-model remediation strategies spanning fifteen families all fail, revealing a persistent independence-accuracy trade-off at the 7B--32B scale: methods that increase trace independence destroy accuracy (Debate: $\\kappa = 0.15$, $-18.6$\\,pp), while methods preserving accuracy cannot break the correlation floor ($\\kappa \\geq 0.40$ across three model families).\nOracle controls confirm the aggregation mechanism is sound ($+29.90$\\,pp and $+14.22$\\,pp on FOLIO, $+11.50$\\,pp ProofWriter; $p < 0.001$); same-model self-extraction captures only ${\\sim}$12\\% of the oracle ceiling.\nHowever, a cross-architecture proof-of-concept---DeBERTa-large NLI combined with SC---yields $+3.50$\\,pp on ProofWriter ($p = 0.013$; $+10.67$\\,pp at $w{=}5$, $p < 0.001$), confirmed across three NLI architectures, suggesting that the oracle gap is partially recoverable when verification signal comes from outside the reasoning model's distribution.\n",
        "numLines": 9,
        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 9
      }
    },
    "displayPath": "../../../docs/paper/abstract.tex"
  },

- The SICA (Symbolic Invariant Consensus Aggregation) pipeline with constraint extraction, deduplication, and MAX-SAT solving

1	\section{Analysis}
2	\label{sec:analysis}
3	
4	This section tests the three predictions of the Symbolic Mirage hypothesis (\S\ref{sec:mirage}): trace correlation and $K_{\text{eff}}$ collapse (\S\ref{sec:effective-k}), systematic confirmation bias with $\text{BR} > 1$ across architectures (\S\ref{sec:confirmation-bias}), process-level independence (\S\ref{sec:process-kappa}), supporting diagnostics (\S\ref{sec:diagnostics}), and the inescapability of the mirage under twenty-plus same-model interventions (\S\ref{sec:remediation}).
5	
6	\subsection{Condorcet Effective-K}
7	\label{sec:effective-k}
8	
9	Table~\ref{tab:independence} reports Fleiss' $\kappa$ and Effective-K (Eq.~\ref{eq:effk}) across 15 conditions at $K = 12$. Among instruction-tuned models, $\kappa$ ranges from 0.21 to 0.82, yielding $K_{\text{eff}} = 1.20\text{--}3.62$. The four Mistral-7B FOLIO seeds produce $\kappa \in [0.41, 0.51]$, yet $\Delta$ ranges from $+3.43$ to $-0.49$\,pp---seed-level constraint quality, not independence alone, appears to influence the variation. Two conditions are jointly necessary for positive $\Delta$: sufficient $K_{\text{eff}}$ ($\leq 1.30$ never improves) and sufficient SC accuracy ($K_{\text{eff}} = 3.62$ with SC $= 39\%$ produces significant degradation). Cross-model validation confirms universality: all four families produce $\kappa \geq 0.41$ (Table~\ref{tab:kappa_generalization}; Appendix~\ref{app:cross-model-kappa}).
10	
11	\begin{table}[t]
12	\centering
13	\small
14	\caption{Trace independence metrics ($K = 12$, sorted by $\kappa$ descending). $\kappa$: Fleiss' Kappa. $K_{\text{eff}}$: Effective-K (Eq.~\ref{eq:effk}). SC: Self-Consistency baseline. $\Delta$: SICA $-$ SC (pp). {*}\,$p < 0.05$; positive $\Delta$ values do not reach significance. multi-T $= T \in \{0.3, 0.7, 1.2\}$, 4 traces each.}
15	\label{tab:independence}
16	\begin{tabular}{@{}llccrr@{}}
17	\toprule
18	Model & Condition & $\kappa$ & $K_{\text{eff}}$ & SC (\%) & $\Delta$ (pp) \\
19	\midrule
20	Qwen3 (think) & FOLIO & 0.91 & 1.09 & ${\sim}$84 & ${\sim}$0 \\
21	Qwen2.5-14B & LogiQA & 0.82 & 1.20 & 78.50 & $-$0.50 \\
22	Qwen3 & FOLIO & 0.77 & 1.26 & 84.80 & $-$0.49 \\
23	Qwen2.5-14B & FOLIO & 0.74 & 1.30 & 75.49 & $-$0.98 \\
24	Qwen2.5-7B & FOLIO & 0.66 & 1.46 & 75.98 & $+$0.49 \\
25	\addlinespace
26	Mistral-7B ($T{=}0.3$) & FOLIO & 0.53 & 1.77 & 58.33 & $-$1.47 \\
27	Mistral-7B & LogiQA & 0.51 & 1.81 & 51.00 & $+$0.50 \\
28	Mistral-7B (s456) & FOLIO & 0.51 & 1.82 & 55.88 & 0.00 \\
29	Mistral-7B & FOLIO & 0.50 & 1.84 & 55.88 & $+$3.43 \\
30	Mistral-7B (s123) & FOLIO & 0.49 & 1.88 & 54.41 & $+$2.94 \\
31	Mistral-7B (s789) & FOLIO & 0.41 & 2.17 & 54.41 & $-$0.49 \\
32	Mistral-7B ($T{=}0.5$) & FOLIO & 0.52 & 1.80 & 58.33 & $-$0.98 \\
33	Mistral-7B (multi-T) & FOLIO & 0.48 & 1.91 & 57.84 & 0.00 \\
34	Mistral-7B ($T{=}1.0$) & FOLIO & 0.48 & 1.91 & 55.39 & 0.00 \\
35	Mistral-7B & PW-D5 & 0.21 & 3.62 & 39.33 & $\mathbf{-2.83}${*} \\
36	\bottomrule
37	\end{tabular}
38	\end{table}
39	
40	
41	\subsection{Symbolic Confirmation Bias}
42	\label{sec:confirmation-bias}
43	
44	The trace correlation above manifests through a specific mechanism that explains \emph{all three} regime failures: self-extracted constraints systematically confirm the model's majority answer regardless of correctness. On Mistral-7B FOLIO ($n = 204$), when SICA errs, 83.3\% of extracted constraints support the incorrect majority answer---a bias ratio of $4.97\times$ (pooled raw ratio for Mistral-7B FOLIO default seed; $4.24\times$ when MaxSAT-weighted; per-model ratios in Table~\ref{tab:cross-model-bias}) compared to correct problems. Consequently, 94\% of SICA errors overlap with SC errors: symbolic aggregation adds no independent corrective signal. The MAX-SAT solver rejects only 3.4\% of constraints on average ($|\mathcal{C}^{\setminus}| / |\mathcal{C}|$, $p = 0.636$ between correct/incorrect), confirming degeneration (Eq.~\ref{eq:degenerate}). Counterintuitively, jointly-wrong problems produce \emph{more} constraints (41.9 vs.\ 37.1 for correct): higher symbolic density amplifies rather than corrects bias.
45	
46	The causal chain is: the model generates an answer, then extracts FOL consistent with it. Constraints use trace-specific variable names (56.4\% zero cross-answer variable overlap), so MAX-SAT retains nearly all and aggregation reduces to weighted majority voting (Eq.~\ref{eq:degenerate}). A premise-only experiment isolates the bias source: filtering conclusion-referencing constraints (23\% of total) halves the effect ($+2.45$\,pp, $p = 0.359$, n.s.). Confirmation bias generalizes across families: bias ratios of $4.97\times$ (Mistral), $3.93\times$ (Qwen-14B), and $2.88\times$ (LLaMA) all exceed $1.0$ (Table~\ref{tab:cross-model-bias}), with the gradient aligning to regime boundaries. Error analysis reveals 64\% of joint SICA+SC errors predict \textsc{Unknown} for \textsc{True}/\textsc{False} problems, and 30\% are confident systematic errors where all 12 traces agree on the wrong answer (Appendix~\ref{app:error-categorization}). Bias intensifies along reasoning steps: late-step constraints (step $\geq 7$) exhibit 27.2\% conclusion leakage vs.\ 22.0\% for early steps (Fisher $p = 3 \times 10^{-6}$), and SICA restricted to late-only constraints degrades by $-10.3$\,pp while early-only gains $+2.0$\,pp.
47	
48	
49	\subsection{Process-Level Independence}
50	\label{sec:process-kappa}
51	
52	Answer-level $\kappa$ may underestimate the independence bottleneck: two traces can follow nearly identical reasoning yet reach different final answers, causing $\kappa$ to treat them as disagreeing. We define process similarity as the mean pairwise cosine similarity of TF-IDF trace representations:
53	\begin{equation}
54	\label{eq:process-sim}
55	\text{ProcessSim}(q) = \frac{2}{K(K{-}1)} \sum_{i < j} \cos(\mathbf{v}_i^q, \mathbf{v}_j^q),
56	\end{equation}
57	where $\mathbf{v}_i^q$ is the TF-IDF vector of trace $t_i$ for question $q$. On Mistral-7B FOLIO ($n = 204$), mean ProcessSim is $0.69$, far exceeding answer-level $\kappa = 0.41$. The process--answer correlation is model-strength dependent: $r = -0.06$ ($p = 0.36$) on Mistral-7B, where answer diversity does not reflect reasoning diversity (Figure~\ref{fig:process-kappa}), rising to $r = +0.24$ ($p < 0.001$) on Qwen2.5-14B. This pattern generalizes across four families (Table~\ref{tab:cross-model-process}): the cosine--$\kappa$ gap narrows from $0.23$ (Mistral) to $0.02$ (Qwen-14B), meaning answer-level $\kappa$ already captures the bottleneck for strong models, while process-$\kappa$ reveals hidden homogeneity in weaker models where answer diversity masks process convergence. In all families, interventions targeting answer diversity (temperature scaling, diverse prompting) cannot alter the underlying reasoning paths.
58	
59	\begin{figure}[t]
60	\centering
61	\includegraphics[width=0.85\columnwidth]{figures/fig3_process_kappa_scatter.pdf}
62	\vspace{-2mm}
63	\caption{Answer agreement vs.\ CoT cosine similarity (Mistral-7B, FOLIO, $n{=}204$). Flat regression ($r = -0.06$, $p = 0.36$); stronger models show significant positive correlation ($r = +0.24$, Qwen-14B; Table~\ref{tab:cross-model-process}).}
64	\label{fig:process-kappa}
65	\end{figure}
66	
67	\subsection{Supporting Diagnostics}
68	\label{sec:diagnostics}
69	
70	Further diagnostics---constraint ablation, discriminativeness, entropy decomposition, and hyperparameter sensitivity---confirm the mechanism (Appendices~\ref{app:extraction}--\ref{app:entropy}).
71	
72	
73	\subsection{Remediation Analysis}
74	\label{sec:remediation}
75	\label{sec:interventions}
76	
77	We test more than twenty-five remediation strategies spanning prompt engineering, method-level redesign, and fifteen additional directions. All produce neutral or negative results.
78	
79	\paragraph{Prompt-level interventions.} Nine strategies spanning six dimensions (Appendix Table~\ref{tab:remediation}) all fail: contrastive prompting reduces non-discriminative constraints but $\Delta = -1.1$\,pp; cross-model extraction yields accuracy within $\pm 1$\,pp; multi-temperature ($T \in \{0.3, 0.7, 1.2\}$) yields $\Delta = 0.00$\,pp.
80	
81	\paragraph{Method-level interventions.} ICG ($\Delta \approx 0$, $p = .206$), Contrastive ($\Delta = -9.31$\,pp, $p < 0.01$), and Debate ($\Delta = -18.63$/$-23.00$\,pp, $p < 0.001$) all fail; three of six conditions reach significance, all negative (Table~\ref{tab:method_interventions}, Appendix~\ref{app:remediation}).
82	
83	\paragraph{Additional directions.} Fifteen further experiments---code verification, iterative refinement, logprob filtering, cross-model ensembles, premise-only filtering, DPP trace selection, Z3 strict-mode, and alternative objectives---all produce neutral-to-negative results (Appendix Table~\ref{tab:additional_interventions}).
84	
85	\paragraph{Independence-accuracy trade-off.} Table~\ref{tab:kappa_generalization} (Appendix) compares $\kappa$ across four aggregation methods and three model families. SC, Diverse-Prompt, and ICG cluster at $\kappa \approx 0.40$--$0.47$; Debate reaches $\kappa = 0.15$ but collapses accuracy by $-18.6$\,pp. All families cluster above $\kappa = 0.40$, confirming a same-model ceiling: methods that increase independence destroy accuracy, and vice versa.
```

1	\section{Related Work}
2	\label{sec:related-work}
3	
4	\paragraph{Self-Consistency and extensions.}
5	SC selects the most frequent answer across chain-of-thought traces \citep{wang2023sc}. Extensions include universal SC for free-form generation \citep{chen2024usc}, confidence weighting \citep{taubenfeld2025cisc}, diverse prompting with step-aware verification \citep{li2023diverse}, program-based vote filtering \citep{toh2024notallvotes}, and reasoning-aware filtering \citep{wan2024rasc, kim2026reasc}. \citet{loo2025reevaluating} show that SC gains plateau early. Our Condorcet analysis explains this: $\kappa = 0.21$--$0.82$ compresses $K_{\text{eff}}$ to 1.20--3.62 for $K = 12$ (\S\ref{sec:effective-k}).
6	
7	\paragraph{Neuro-symbolic reasoning.}
8	Logic-LM \citep{pan2023logiclm} and LINC \citep{olausson2023linc} translate problems into FOL for external provers; LINC documents ${\sim}$40\% translation errors. Recent extensions---CLOVER \citep{ryu2025clover}, DyLoS \citep{xu2026dylos}, Aristotle \citep{li2025aristotle}, FoVer \citep{wang2025fover}, VERGE \citep{singh2026verge}, LogicAgent \citep{luo2025logicagent}---add compositional translation, solver selection, decomposition, trained verifiers, and formal refinement, each introducing capabilities beyond the reasoning model. \citet{amonkar2025realitycheck} show that LLM formalization degrades with complexity; \citet{chan2026position} argue that logical soundness is not a reliable criterion for neurosymbolic verification with LLMs. Our oracle controls confirm the mechanism is sound ($+29.90$/$+14.22$\,pp FOLIO, $+11.50$\,pp ProofWriter); the failure localizes to self-extraction quality (\S\ref{sec:oracle}).
9	
10	\paragraph{Test-time compute and verification.}
11	Scaling test-time computation through additional traces \citep{snell2025scaling} faces the same correlation challenge: same-model attempts collapse into correlated solutions. Reasoning models (o1, DeepSeek-R1 \citep{guo2025deepseekr1}, QwQ \citep{qwen2024qwq}) extend thinking time but remain subject to the independence bottleneck: QwQ-32B produces $\kappa = 0.91$ and $C_0 = 2.6\%$, suggesting that longer reasoning chains intensify homogenization rather than alleviating it. Trained verifiers offer an alternative \citep{cobbe2021gsm8k, lightman2024prm, wang2024mathshepherd}; multi-agent debate refines answers through discussion \citep{liang2023encouraging, du2023improving}. Self-correction without external feedback degrades performance \citep{huang2024selfcorrect, stechly2025selfverification}, paralleling our confirmation bias finding (\S\ref{sec:confirmation-bias}).
12	
13	\paragraph{CJT and ensemble diversity.}
14	CJT \citep{condorcet1785} guarantees convergence under independent voting; \citet{ladha1992correlated} prove correlated voters reduce effective ensemble size; \citet{lefort2024condorcet_llm} apply CJT to LLM classification. We extend this by quantifying \emph{why} self-correction fails---same-model traces are correlated voters ($K_{\text{eff}}$ collapses)---and showing this persists even under formal verification (Z3-checked FOL) and at the process level (\S\ref{sec:process-kappa}).
```

</details>

```text
1	\section{Experiments}
2	\label{sec:experiments}
3	
4	\subsection{Setup}
5	\label{sec:exp-setup}
6	
7	We evaluate SICA against Self-Consistency (SC) across six models on three reasoning domains with over 3,000 condition-evaluations across ${\sim}$1,250 unique instances. Beyond accuracy comparisons, we conduct diagnostic analyses of symbolic confirmation bias and process-level trace independence (\S\ref{sec:analysis}).
8	
9	\paragraph{Models.} Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct, Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, Qwen3-14B (non-thinking mode)~\citep{yang2024qwen25}, Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3~\citep{jiang2023mistral}, LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct~\citep{dubey2024llama3}, and QwQ-32B~\citep{qwen2024qwq} (7B--32B parameters).
10	
11	\paragraph{Datasets.} FOLIO~\citep{han2022folio} ($n{=}204$, with gold FOL annotations), ProofWriter depth-5~\citep{tafjord2021proofwriter} ($n{=}600$), LogiQA~\citep{liu2020logiqa} ($n{=}200$). StrategyQA~\citep{geva2021strategyqa} ($n{=}200$) serves as a domain-formalizability diagnostic. GSM8K~\citep{cobbe2021gsm8k} ($n{=}30$) and AIME 2024 ($n{=}22$) probe the degenerate regime ($C_0 < 5\%$).
12	
13	\paragraph{Parameters.} $K{=}12$ traces at $T{=}0.7$; extraction at $T_{\text{ext}}{=}0.3$. Z3 timeout: 10s. $\alpha{=}0.5$. Statistical significance via McNemar exact test ($\alpha{=}0.05$); 95\% bootstrap CIs from 10,000 resamples for $n \geq 200$.
14	
15	\paragraph{Computational cost.} On Mistral-7B FOLIO ($K{=}12$), SICA takes $4.5{\times}$ SC wall-clock time; constraint extraction ($K$ additional LLM calls) accounts for 76\% of overhead, Z3 solving is negligible (${<}0.1\%$).
16	
17	
18	\subsection{Main Results}
19	\label{sec:main-results}
20	
21	Table~\ref{tab:main_results} reports accuracy across all conditions sorted by SC baseline. We identify three empirical regimes (thresholds observation-driven, not theoretically derived). SICA effectiveness depends sharply on this accuracy regime.
22	
23	\begin{table*}[t]
24	\centering
25	\small
26	\caption{SICA vs.\ SC across all conditions sorted by SC accuracy. Results hold across the full capability range: from Mistral-7B (54.41\% SC) to Qwen3-14B thinking mode (86.27\% SC), SICA delivers no significant gain. $\Delta$ = SICA $-$ SC (pp). $^b$In-context learning with gold FOL examples. $\dagger$\,$n < 100$ (suggestive only). \textbf{Bold $\Delta$}: $p < 0.05$ (McNemar). {*}\,$p < 0.05$;\enspace{**}\,$p < 0.01$;\enspace{***}\,$p < 0.001$. Failure modes detailed in Appendix~\ref{app:failure-modes}.}
27	\label{tab:main_results}
28	\begin{tabular}{@{}llrrrrl@{}}
29	\toprule
30	Model & Domain & $n$ & SC (\%) & SICA (\%) & $\Delta$ (pp) & $p$ \\
31	\midrule
32	\multicolumn{7}{l}{\textit{Type C: error amplification (SC $\leq$ 40\%)}} \\
33	Mistral-7B & PW-D5 & 600 & 39.33 & 36.50 & $\mathbf{-2.83}$ & 0.014{*} \\
34	\midrule
35	\multicolumn{7}{l}{\textit{Intermediate SC (SC $\sim$50--55\%, Mistral-7B only)}} \\
36	Mistral-7B & LogiQA & 200 & 51.00 & 51.50 & $+$0.50 & 1.000 \\
37	Mistral-7B & FOLIO (s123) & 204 & 54.41 & 57.35 & $+$2.94 & 0.070 \\
38	Mistral-7B & FOLIO & 204 & 55.88 & 59.31 & $+$3.43 & 0.092 \\
39	Mistral-7B & FOLIO (s456) & 204 & 55.88 & 55.88 & 0.00 & 1.000 \\
40	Mistral-7B & FOLIO (s789) & 204 & 54.41 & 53.92 & $-$0.49 & 1.000 \\
41	\midrule

43	LLaMA-3.1-8B & FOLIO & 204 & 66.18 & 61.76 & $-$4.41 & 0.093 \\
44	LLaMA-3.1-8B & StrategyQA & 200 & 72.00 & 74.00 & $+$2.00 & 0.424 \\
45	Qwen2.5-14B & FOLIO (ICL)$^b$ & 199 & 74.37 & 74.87 & $+$0.50 & 1.000 \\
46	Qwen2.5-14B & PW-D5 & 600 & 75.17 & 72.67 & $\mathbf{-2.50}$ & 0.011{*} \\
47	Qwen2.5-14B & FOLIO & 204 & 75.49 & 74.51 & $-$0.98 & 1.000 \\
48	Qwen2.5-7B & FOLIO & 204 & 75.98 & 76.47 & $+$0.49 & 1.000 \\
49	Qwen2.5-14B & LogiQA & 200 & 78.50 & 78.00 & $-$0.50 & 1.000 \\
50	Qwen3-14B & FOLIO & 204 & 84.80 & 84.31 & $-$0.49 & 1.000 \\
51	Qwen3-14B & PW-D5 & 600 & 85.00 & 85.67 & $+$0.67 & 1.000 \\
52	Qwen3-14B (think) & FOLIO & 204 & 86.27 & 86.76 & $+$0.49 & 1.000 \\
53	\midrule
54	\multicolumn{7}{l}{\textit{Degenerate ($C_0 < 5\%$)}} \\
55	QwQ-32B & AIME 2024$^\dagger$ & 22 & 72.70 & 72.70 & 0.00 & \textrm{---} \\
56	Qwen2.5-14B & GSM8K$^\dagger$ & 30 & 96.67 & 96.67 & 0.00 & \textrm{---} \\
57	\midrule
58	\multicolumn{7}{l}{\textit{Oracle upper bound (gold FOL/DSL + Z3)}} \\
59	Mistral-7B & FOLIO & 204 & 55.88 & \textbf{85.78} & $\mathbf{+29.90}$ & $1.0{\times}10^{-13}${***} \\
60	Qwen2.5-14B & FOLIO & 204 & 75.49 & \textbf{89.71} & $\mathbf{+14.22}$ & $2.0{\times}10^{-7}${***} \\
61	Qwen2.5-14B & ProofWriter & 600 & 75.17 & \textbf{86.67} & $\mathbf{+11.50}$ & $9.21{\times}10^{-15}${***} \\
62	\bottomrule
63	\end{tabular}
64	\end{table*}
65	
66	\paragraph{Intermediate-SC region.} Mistral-7B on FOLIO (SC $\sim$50--55\%) shows positive directional trends in 2 of 4 seeds ($\Delta = +3.43$\,pp, $p = 0.092$; $+2.94$\,pp, $p = 0.070$); no seed reaches $p < 0.05$ (4-seed mean $+1.47$\,pp). Seeds with identical SC baselines (55.88\%) diverge ($+3.43$ vs.\ $0.00$\,pp), suggesting seed-level constraint quality matters (Appendix~\ref{app:entropy}). Mistral-7B on LogiQA ($\Delta = +0.50$\,pp, $p = 1.0$) shows the same SC range is insufficient when the domain resists formalization (Appendix~\ref{app:failure-modes}).
67	
68	\paragraph{Type A (SC $\geq$ 60\%): confirmation bias.} All nine Type~A conditions (SC $\geq 60\%$, combined $N > 2{,}800$) produce no significant positive effects. The largest positive trend is LLaMA-3.1-8B on StrategyQA ($\Delta = +2.00$\,pp, $p = 0.424$, n.s.), while Qwen2.5-14B on ProofWriter shows significant degradation ($\Delta = -2.50$\,pp, $p = 0.011$). Even in-context learning with gold FOL examples ($\Delta = +0.50$\,pp, n.s.) cannot break confirmation bias.
69	
70	\paragraph{Type C (SC $\leq$ 40\%): error amplification.} Mistral-7B on ProofWriter ($n = 600$, SC $= 39.33\%$) shows $\Delta = -2.83$\,pp ($p = 0.014$): SICA \emph{significantly hurts} by formalizing incorrect reasoning.
71	
72	\paragraph{Degenerate cases.} When $C_0 < 5\%$, nearly all traces agree and SICA reduces to SC by construction.
73	
74	\paragraph{Equivalence testing.} TOST equivalence tests \citep{lakens2017equivalence} with margin $\pm 2$\,pp establish equivalence for 4 of 15 non-degenerate conditions; 5 show genuine SICA--SC differences (4 negative, 1 positive n.s.); the remaining 6 are underpowered (Appendix~\ref{app:tost}).
75	
76	
77	\subsection{Oracle Control}
78	\label{sec:oracle}
79	
80	Oracle variants replace self-extracted constraints with gold annotations: FOL from FOLIO ($n = 204$) and DSL from ProofWriter ($n = 600$). Oracle-SICA yields $+29.90$\,pp on Mistral-7B FOLIO ($p < 10^{-13}$), $+14.22$\,pp on Qwen2.5-14B FOLIO ($p = 2.0 \times 10^{-7}$), and $+11.50$\,pp on ProofWriter ($p < 10^{-14}$). Z3 agrees with gold labels on 95.1\% (FOLIO) and 97.1\% (ProofWriter) of problems. The MAX-SAT mechanism is sound; Mistral-7B's best self-extracted result ($+3.43$\,pp) captures only 11.5\% of its oracle ceiling, with ${\sim}$88\% lost to extraction quality.
81	
82	
83

\\\\\nQwen2.5-14B & GSM8K$^\\dagger$ & 30 & 96.67 & 96.67 & 0.00 & \\textrm{---} \\\\\n\\midrule\n\\multicolumn{7}{l}{\\textit{Oracle upper bound (gold FOL/DSL + Z3)}} \\\\\nMistral-7B & FOLIO & 204 & 55.88 & \\textbf{85.78} & $\\mathbf{+29.90}$ & $1.0{\\times}10^{-13}${***} \\\\\nQwen2.5-14B & FOLIO & 204 & 75.49 & \\textbf{89.71} & $\\mathbf{+14.22}$ & $2.0{\\times}10^{-7}${***} \\\\\nQwen2.5-14B & ProofWriter & 600 & 75.17 & \\textbf{86.67} & $\\mathbf{+11.50}$ & $9.21{\\times}10^{-15}${***} \\\\\n\\bottomrule\n\\end{tabular}\n\\end{table*}\n\n\\paragraph{Intermediate-SC region.} Mistral-7B on FOLIO (SC $\\sim$50--55\\%) shows positive directional trends in 2 of 4 seeds ($\\Delta = +3.43$\\,pp, $p = 0.092$; $+2.94$\\,pp, $p = 0.070$); no seed reaches $p < 0.05$ (4-seed mean $+1.47$\\,pp). Seeds with identical SC baselines (55.88\\%) diverge ($+3.43$ vs.\\ $0.00$\\,pp), suggesting seed-level constraint quality matters (Appendix~\\ref{app:entropy}). Mistral-7B on LogiQA ($\\Delta = +0.50$\\,pp, $p = 1.0$) shows the same SC range is insufficient when the domain resists formalization (Appendix~\\ref{app:failure-modes}).\n\n\\paragraph{Type A (SC $\\geq$ 60\\%): confirmation bias.} All nine Type~A conditions (SC $\\geq 60\\%$, combined $N > 2{,}800$) produce no significant positive effects. The largest positive trend is LLaMA-3.1-8B on StrategyQA ($\\Delta = +2.00$\\,pp, $p = 0.424$, n.s.), while Qwen2.5-14B on ProofWriter shows significant degradation ($\\Delta = -2.50$\\,pp, $p = 0.011$). Even in-context learning with gold FOL examples ($\\Delta = +0.50$\\,pp, n.s.) cannot break confirmation bias.\n\n\\paragraph{Type C (SC $\\leq$ 40\\%): error amplification.} Mistral-7B on ProofWriter ($n = 600$, SC $= 39.33\\%$) shows $\\Delta = -2.83$\\,pp ($p = 0.014$): SICA \\emph{significantly hurts} by formalizing incorrect reasoning.\n\n\\paragraph{Degenerate cases.} When $C_0 < 5\\%$, nearly all traces agree and SICA reduces to SC by construction.\n\n\\paragraph{Equivalence testing.} TOST equivalence tests \\citep{lakens2017equivalence} with margin $\\pm 2$\\,pp establish equivalence for 4 of 15 non-degenerate conditions; 5 show genuine SICA--SC differences (4 negative, 1 positive n.s.); the remaining 6 are underpowered (Appendix~\\ref{app:tost}).\n\n\n\\subsection{Oracle Control}\n\\label{sec:oracle}\n\nOracle variants replace self-extracted constraints with gold annotations: FOL from FOLIO ($n = 204$) and DSL from ProofWriter ($n = 600$). Oracle-SICA yields $+29.90$\\,pp on Mistral-7B FOLIO ($p < 10^{-13}$), $+14.22$\\,pp on Qwen2.5-14B FOLIO ($p = 2.0 \\times 10^{-7}$), and $+11.50$\\,pp on ProofWriter ($p < 10^{-14}$). Z3 agrees with gold labels on 95.1\\% (FOLIO) and 97.1\\% (ProofWriter) of problems. The MAX-SAT mechanism is sound; Mistral-7B's best self-extracted result ($+3.43$\\,pp) captures only 11.5\\% of its oracle ceiling, with ${\\sim}$88\\% lost to extraction quality.\n\n\n",

In the discussion section, I should emphasize that even the simpler propositional constraints are failing, which suggests full first-order logic wouldn't help either. This is medium effort but low risk since it strengthens the paper's argument.

For R8-N2, I'm planning to run the DeBERTa combination approach on the Qwen3-14B traces we already have for both FOLIO and ProofWriter datasets to strengthen the cross-architecture proof of concept.  I'm checking whether we already have the necessary results — it looks like we do have Qwen3-14B ProofWriter DeBERTa results showing no benefit due to the capability gap, which actually confirms the paper's point even though the results are negative. The challenge is that these negative results don't directly support the main claims, but they do validate the capability gap explanation for why the approach fails across different generators. Looking at the NLI Matrix results, I see we have data for Qwen3 on ProofWriter that isn't currently in the paper — just +0.50pp, non-significant, but it completes the picture alongside the Qwen3 FOLIO results already mentioned. I should add this to strengthen the capability gap argument across both datasets. to strengthen the multi-generator evidence.

So the paper currently focuses on Mistral-7B as the primary generator. The reviewer concern is valid — we only use Mistral as generator in the combo. But we DO have Qwen3 data showing the capability gap boundary.

The fix: explicitly integrate the Qwen3 generator results into §6 to show both Mistral-7B (weak generator with +3.50pp ProofWriter and +2.94pp FOLIO gains) and Qwen3-14B (strong generator with negligible improvements at +0.50pp ProofWriter and +0.49pp FOLIO), which demonstrates the capability gap boundary and confirms we're testing across multiple generators rather than just one. I could also test Qwen2.5-14B as a middle ground if those traces are available, though the priority is making the existing multi-generator evidence more explicit in §6. For the semantic dedup issue, I should extract the pre- and post-dedup constraint counts from the existing data and quantify the impact in the limitations section, noting that a full Z3 equivalence check isn't feasible within the timeline.

The abstract needs updating to specify "open-source LLMs (7B-32B)" rather than claiming full capability coverage, and acknowledge the math domain as exploratory—though this requires explicit approval since the abstract is locked.

I should also scope the 91.8% constraint quality claim explicitly to Qwen2.5-14B in the text rather than implying broader generalization.

In the discussion, I want to connect this work to Turpin et al.'s findings on CoT faithfulness, framing extracted constraints as potentially inheriting representation bias rather than genuine reasoning—this provides a mechanistic explanation for the symbolic confirmation bias we're observing.
